# 逐文件代码与小白讲解

按各部分教程的“跟写顺序”写；本页按文件列出新增或修改源码，供搜索和对照。固定写法是库规定的接口；项目名称能改，但相关调用处要一起改。

路径相对于对应参考工程。例如06-knowledge/backend/app/models.py应对照写入E:\oshino\backend\app\models.py。这里只列变化，完整快照中的其他文件沿用上一部分。空文件只需创建。

> 说明：08-evaluation 的最终前端已按设计图改版，新增和修改的文件见 [工作台界面改版](15-工作台界面改版.md)。本页前面保留各阶段版本；完成第八部分后，继续跟写本页末尾“最终工作台界面”中的完整源码，最终版本对应 `08-evaluation/frontend`。后端相似案例响应也增加了 `module_id`，同样见该章。

## 第六部分

### 修改：backend/app/integrations/case_index.py

`backend/app/integrations/case_index.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 继续提供原来的CaseIndex调用方式，把具体集合和主键交给共享实现。

**固定写法：** super().__init__调用父类初始化。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 保留ticket_cases、ticket_id和原元数据文件，已有第五部分索引可以继续读取。

<!-- source: 06-knowledge/backend/app/integrations/case_index.py -->
```python
from pathlib import Path

from app.integrations.hybrid_index import HybridIndex, IndexNotReady  # noqa: F401

COLLECTION = "ticket_cases"


class CaseIndex(HybridIndex):
    def __init__(self, path: Path, model: str, base_url: str, dimensions: int):
        super().__init__(path, model, base_url, dimensions, COLLECTION, "ticket_id", Path(str(path) + ".index.json"))
```

### 新增：backend/app/integrations/hybrid_index.py

`backend/app/integrations/hybrid_index.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 案例和知识都需要建集合、存向量、判断就绪、加载和搜索，因此共享这些实际重复的逻辑。

**固定写法：** MilvusClient、add_field、load_collection、AnnSearchRequest、RRFRanker是库API。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 集合名、主键和元数据路径由两个具体索引传入；第八部分增加mode用于真实对比，默认仍是混合查询。

<!-- source: 06-knowledge/backend/app/integrations/hybrid_index.py -->
```python
import asyncio
import json
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path

from pymilvus import AnnSearchRequest, DataType, Function, FunctionType, MilvusClient, RRFRanker


class IndexNotReady(RuntimeError):
    """缺少完整索引，或当前配置与构建时不一致。"""


class HybridIndex:
    def __init__(
        self,
        path: Path,
        model: str,
        base_url: str,
        dimensions: int,
        collection: str,
        primary_key: str,
        metadata_path: Path,
    ):
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.collection = collection
        self.primary_key = primary_key
        self.metadata_path = metadata_path
        self.signature = {"model": model, "base_url": base_url.rstrip("/"), "dimensions": dimensions}
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.client = MilvusClient(str(path), timeout=10.0)

    async def __aenter__(self):
        return self

    async def __aexit__(self, exc_type, exc_value, traceback):
        await asyncio.to_thread(self.client.close)

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_value, traceback):
        self.client.close()

    def ready(self) -> dict:
        if not self.metadata_path.is_file():
            raise IndexNotReady("检索索引尚未完整建立，请运行重建命令")
        metadata = json.loads(self.metadata_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if any(metadata[key] != value for key, value in self.signature.items()):
            raise IndexNotReady("Embedding 模型、地址或维度已变更，请重建索引")
        if not self.client.has_collection(self.collection):
            raise IndexNotReady("索引元数据存在，但检索集合缺失，请重建索引")
        return metadata

    def reset(self) -> None:
        # 仅在后端停止后重建派生索引。先撤销就绪标记，失败时不能查询半套数据。
        self.metadata_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        if self.client.has_collection(self.collection):
            self.client.drop_collection(self.collection)
        schema = self.client.create_schema(auto_id=False, enable_dynamic_field=False)
        schema.add_field(self.primary_key, DataType.INT64, is_primary=True)
        schema.add_field(
            "content", DataType.VARCHAR, max_length=65535, enable_analyzer=True, analyzer_params={"tokenizer": "jieba"}
        )
        schema.add_field("dense_vector", DataType.FLOAT_VECTOR, dim=self.dimensions)
        schema.add_field("sparse_vector", DataType.SPARSE_FLOAT_VECTOR)
        for field in ["product_id", "module_id", "category_id"]:
            schema.add_field(field, DataType.INT64)
        schema.add_field("fingerprint", DataType.VARCHAR, max_length=64)
        schema.add_function(
            Function(
                name="content_bm25",
                function_type=FunctionType.BM25,
                input_field_names=["content"],
                output_field_names=["sparse_vector"],
            )
        )
        indexes = self.client.prepare_index_params()
        indexes.add_index("dense_vector", index_type="FLAT", metric_type="COSINE")
        indexes.add_index("sparse_vector", index_type="SPARSE_INVERTED_INDEX", metric_type="BM25")
        self.client.create_collection(self.collection, schema=schema, index_params=indexes)

    def add(self, rows: list[dict]) -> None:
        self.client.insert(self.collection, rows)

    def finish(self, count: int) -> None:
        self.client.flush(self.collection)
        metadata = {**self.signature, "count": count, "built_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()}
        temporary = self.metadata_path.with_suffix(".tmp")
        temporary.write_text(json.dumps(metadata, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
        temporary.replace(self.metadata_path)

    def search(self, text: str, vector: list[float], expression: str) -> list[dict]:
        self.ready()
        # 索引由独立命令构建；新进程打开已有集合后，需要加载才能检索。
        self.client.load_collection(self.collection)
        requests = [
            AnnSearchRequest([vector], "dense_vector", {"metric_type": "COSINE"}, limit=20, expr=expression),
            AnnSearchRequest([text], "sparse_vector", {"metric_type": "BM25"}, limit=20, expr=expression),
        ]
        return self.client.hybrid_search(
            self.collection,
            reqs=requests,
            ranker=RRFRanker(60),
            limit=20,
            output_fields=["fingerprint"],
        )[0]
```

`backend/app/integrations/knowledge_index.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

class KnowledgeIndex(HybridIndex):

### 修改：backend/app/main.py

> **正式工程接线：** 下面的第六部分代码已对齐当前正式工程的 `create_app`、Settings 和异步会话工厂；保留现有登录保持、密码服务和中间件。


`backend/app/main.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 把本部分的路由挂到已有FastAPI应用上。

**固定写法：** include_router是框架方法，异常处理和生命周期沿用已有实现。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 新增模块的导入和注册要对应；其他部分不用重写。

<!-- source: 06-knowledge/backend/app/main.py -->
```python
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Awaitable, Callable
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from typing import cast
from uuid import uuid4

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, Response
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import async_sessionmaker

from app.core.database import create_database_engine
from app.core.errors import install_error_handlers
from app.core.passwords import PasswordService
from app.core.settings import Settings
from app.modules.analysis.router import router as analysis_router
from app.modules.config.router import router as config_router
from app.modules.knowledge.router import router as knowledge_router
from app.modules.knowledge.search import router as knowledge_search_router
from app.modules.tickets.import_csv import router as import_router
from app.modules.tickets.router import router as tickets_router
from app.modules.users.router import router as users_router


def create_app(settings: Settings | None = None) -> FastAPI:
    @asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(app: FastAPI) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
        config = settings if settings is not None else Settings.load()
        app.state.settings = config
        engine = create_database_engine(config.database_url)
        app.state.session_factory = async_sessionmaker(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
        app.state.passwords = PasswordService()
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            await engine.dispose()

    app = FastAPI(title="售后工单智能辅助系统", lifespan=lifespan)

    @app.middleware("http")
    async def request_id(request: Request, call_next: Callable[[Request], Awaitable[Response]]) -> Response:
        request.state.request_id = uuid4().hex
        response = await call_next(request)
        response.headers["X-Request-ID"] = cast(str, request.state.request_id)
        return response

    install_error_handlers(app)
    app.include_router(users_router)
    app.include_router(config_router)
    app.include_router(import_router)
    app.include_router(tickets_router)
    app.include_router(analysis_router)
    app.include_router(knowledge_router)
    app.include_router(knowledge_search_router)

    @app.get("/api/health", tags=["健康检查"])
    async def health() -> dict[str, str]:
        return {"status": "ok"}

    return app


app = create_app()
```

### 修改：backend/app/models.py

`backend/app/models.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 把知识文档、分块或建议记录映射成数据库表。

**固定写法：** Mapped、mapped_column、ForeignKey、JSON是SQLAlchemy接口。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 表名和字段名是项目约定；改动须配套迁移和调用方，不能只改这个文件。

<!-- source: 06-knowledge/backend/app/models.py -->
```python
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Literal

from sqlalchemy import Boolean, CheckConstraint, DateTime, ForeignKey, String, Text, UniqueConstraint, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship

from app.core.database import Base


class User(Base):
    __tablename__ = "users"
    __table_args__ = (
        CheckConstraint("role IN ('admin', 'staff')", name="users_role_check"),
        UniqueConstraint("username", name="users_username_key"),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    username: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(50))
    display_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100))
    password_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255))
    role: Mapped[Literal["admin", "staff"]] = mapped_column(String(10))
    is_active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())


class Product(Base):
    __tablename__ = "products"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("name", name="products_name_key"),)

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100))
    is_active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True)


class ProductModule(Base):
    __tablename__ = "product_modules"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("product_id", "name", name="modules_product_name_key"),)

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    product_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("products.id", name="product_modules_product_id_fkey"))
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100))
    is_active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True)


class TicketCategory(Base):
    __tablename__ = "ticket_categories"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("name", name="ticket_categories_name_key"),)

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100))
    is_active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True)


class Ticket(Base):
    __tablename__ = "tickets"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("import_key", name="tickets_import_key_key"),
        CheckConstraint("status IN ('pending', 'processing', 'resolved', 'closed')", name="tickets_status_check"),
        CheckConstraint("priority IN ('P1', 'P2', 'P3', 'P4')", name="tickets_priority_check"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status NOT IN ('resolved', 'closed') OR length(trim(solution)) > 0",
            name="tickets_solution_check",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    # 手动新建工单没有来源编号；CSV 记录通过唯一约束防止重复导入。
    import_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(100), nullable=True)
    title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200))
    description: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    customer_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100))
    customer_email: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(320))
    product_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("products.id"), index=True)
    module_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("product_modules.id"), index=True)
    category_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("ticket_categories.id"), index=True)
    owner_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("users.id"), index=True)
    priority: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(2), index=True)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="pending", index=True)
    solution: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    records: Mapped[list["TicketRecord"]] = relationship(order_by="TicketRecord.id", lazy="selectin")

    @property
    def number(self) -> str:
        return f"T{self.id:06d}"


class TicketRecord(Base):
    __tablename__ = "ticket_records"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    ticket_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("tickets.id"), index=True)
    author_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("users.id"))
    kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20))
    content: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())


class KnowledgeDocument(Base):
    __tablename__ = 'knowledge_documents'
    __table_args__ = (CheckConstraint("status IN ('draft','published','disabled')", name='knowledge_status_check'),)
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200))
    content: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    source_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200))
    product_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey('products.id'))
    module_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey('product_modules.id'))
    category_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey('ticket_categories.id'))
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default='draft')
    revision: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(default=1)
    chunk_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(default=0)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())


class KnowledgeChunk(Base):
    __tablename__ = 'knowledge_chunks'
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint('document_id','chunk_order',name='knowledge_chunk_order_key'),)
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    document_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey('knowledge_documents.id'), index=True)
    chunk_order: Mapped[int] = mapped_column()
    content: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    fingerprint: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64))
```

### 新增：backend/app/modules/knowledge/router.py

`backend/app/modules/knowledge/router.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 处理管理员录入、上传、编辑、发布和停用，也给客服提供已发布文档。

**固定写法：** APIRouter、UploadFile、Form、Depends背后的权限别名是框架接法。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 支持的文件类型、大小、状态变化是明确业务规则；发布不直接调用模型，需要再执行索引命令。

<!-- source: 06-knowledge/backend/app/modules/knowledge/router.py -->
```python
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Annotated, Literal

from fastapi import APIRouter, Form, HTTPException, Query, UploadFile
from pydantic import ValidationError
from sqlalchemy import delete, func, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.security import AdminUser, CurrentUser, DbSession
from app.models import KnowledgeChunk, KnowledgeDocument
from app.modules.knowledge.schemas import DocumentInput, DocumentPage, DocumentView
from app.modules.knowledge.text import document_fingerprint, split_text
from app.modules.tickets.service import validate_catalog

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/knowledge", tags=["知识库"])


async def get_document(db: AsyncSession, document_id: int, *, lock: bool = False) -> KnowledgeDocument:
    query = select(KnowledgeDocument).where(KnowledgeDocument.id == document_id)
    if lock:
        query = query.with_for_update()
    document = await db.scalar(query)
    if document is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "知识文档不存在")
    return document


@router.get("", response_model=DocumentPage)
async def list_documents(
    db: DbSession,
    user: CurrentUser,
    page: Annotated[int, Query(ge=1)] = 1,
    q: Annotated[str, Query(max_length=200)] = "",
    status: Literal["draft", "published", "disabled"] | None = None,
):
    filters = [] if user.role == "admin" else [KnowledgeDocument.status == "published"]
    keyword = q.strip()
    if keyword:
        filters.append(
            KnowledgeDocument.title.icontains(keyword, autoescape=True)
            | KnowledgeDocument.content.icontains(keyword, autoescape=True)
        )
    if status is not None:
        filters.append(KnowledgeDocument.status == status)
    total = await db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(KnowledgeDocument).where(*filters))
    items = (
        await db.scalars(
            select(KnowledgeDocument)
            .where(*filters)
            .order_by(KnowledgeDocument.id.desc())
            .offset((page - 1) * 20)
            .limit(20)
        )
    ).all()
    return {"items": items, "total": total, "page": page}


@router.post("", response_model=DocumentView, status_code=201)
async def create_document(body: DocumentInput, db: DbSession, user: AdminUser):
    await validate_catalog(db, body.product_id, body.module_id, body.category_id)
    document = KnowledgeDocument(**body.model_dump(), source_name="手工录入")
    db.add(document)
    await db.commit()
    return document


@router.post("/upload", response_model=DocumentView, status_code=201)
async def upload_document(
    file: UploadFile,
    title: Annotated[str, Form()],
    product_id: Annotated[int, Form(gt=0)],
    module_id: Annotated[int, Form(gt=0)],
    category_id: Annotated[int, Form(gt=0)],
    db: DbSession,
    user: AdminUser,
):
    filename = file.filename or ""
    if Path(filename).suffix.lower() not in {".md", ".txt"}:
        raise HTTPException(422, "本阶段只支持 UTF-8 的 .md 和 .txt 文件")
    raw = await file.read(256 * 1024 + 1)
    if len(raw) > 256 * 1024:
        raise HTTPException(413, "文档不能超过 256 KiB")
    try:
        content = raw.decode("utf-8-sig")
    except UnicodeDecodeError as error:
        raise HTTPException(422, "文档必须使用 UTF-8 编码") from error
    # 上传和直接录入使用同一套边界规则；错误不包含正文。
    try:
        body = DocumentInput(
            title=title, content=content, product_id=product_id, module_id=module_id, category_id=category_id
        )
    except ValidationError as error:
        raise HTTPException(422, "标题须为1—200字，正文须为1—100000字") from error
    await validate_catalog(db, product_id, module_id, category_id)
    document = KnowledgeDocument(**body.model_dump(), source_name=filename.replace("\\", "/").split("/")[-1][:200])
    db.add(document)
    await db.commit()
    return document


@router.get("/{document_id}", response_model=DocumentView)
async def document_detail(document_id: int, db: DbSession, user: CurrentUser):
    document = await get_document(db, document_id)
    if user.role != "admin" and document.status != "published":
        raise HTTPException(403, "该知识文档尚未发布或已停用")
    return document


@router.put("/{document_id}/content", response_model=DocumentView)
async def update_content(document_id: int, body: DocumentInput, db: DbSession, user: AdminUser):
    document = await get_document(db, document_id, lock=True)
    await validate_catalog(db, body.product_id, body.module_id, body.category_id)
    for field, value in body.model_dump().items():
        setattr(document, field, value)
    document.revision += 1
    document.status = "draft"
    document.chunk_count = 0
    document.updated_at = datetime.now(UTC)
    await db.execute(delete(KnowledgeChunk).where(KnowledgeChunk.document_id == document_id))
    await db.commit()
    return document


@router.post("/{document_id}/publish", response_model=DocumentView)
async def publish_document(document_id: int, db: DbSession, user: AdminUser):
    document = await get_document(db, document_id, lock=True)
    if document.status == "published":
        raise HTTPException(409, "文档已经发布；正文变更请先编辑")
    await validate_catalog(db, document.product_id, document.module_id, document.category_id)
    document.revision += 1
    document.status = "published"
    document.updated_at = datetime.now(UTC)
    chunks = split_text(document.content)
    fingerprint = document_fingerprint(document)
    await db.execute(delete(KnowledgeChunk).where(KnowledgeChunk.document_id == document_id))
    db.add_all(
        [
            KnowledgeChunk(document_id=document.id, chunk_order=i + 1, content=text, fingerprint=fingerprint)
            for i, text in enumerate(chunks)
        ]
    )
    document.chunk_count = len(chunks)
    await db.commit()
    return document


@router.post("/{document_id}/disable", response_model=DocumentView)
async def disable_document(document_id: int, db: DbSession, user: AdminUser):
    document = await get_document(db, document_id, lock=True)
    document.status = "disabled"
    document.updated_at = datetime.now(UTC)
    await db.commit()
    return document
```

### 新增：backend/app/modules/knowledge/schemas.py

`backend/app/modules/knowledge/schemas.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 约定录入内容、文档详情和列表返回什么。

**固定写法：** BaseModel、Field、StringConstraints是Pydantic接口。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** title、content、status等字段是业务契约；字数限制可按需求改，但前端和上传规则要同步。

<!-- source: 06-knowledge/backend/app/modules/knowledge/schemas.py -->
```python
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Annotated, Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, StringConstraints

Title = Annotated[str, StringConstraints(strip_whitespace=True, min_length=1, max_length=200)]
Content = Annotated[str, StringConstraints(strip_whitespace=True, min_length=1, max_length=100000)]


class DocumentInput(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    title: Title
    content: Content
    product_id: int = Field(gt=0)
    module_id: int = Field(gt=0)
    category_id: int = Field(gt=0)


class DocumentView(DocumentInput):
    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)
    id: int
    source_name: str
    status: Literal["draft", "published", "disabled"]
    revision: int
    chunk_count: int
    updated_at: datetime


class DocumentPage(BaseModel):
    items: list[DocumentView]
    total: int
    page: int
```

### 新增：backend/app/modules/knowledge/search.py

`backend/app/modules/knowledge/search.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 工单按模块优先、分类补充查知识片段，并核对文档仍已发布且版本匹配。

**固定写法：** response_model、Depends声明输出和依赖。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 返回的是最多五个块，可能属于同一篇文档。字段名与前端knowledge.ts对应；不能把索引失败改成空列表。

<!-- source: 06-knowledge/backend/app/modules/knowledge/search.py -->
```python
import asyncio
from typing import Annotated, Literal

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.retrieval_settings import RetrievalSettings
from app.core.security import CurrentUser, DbSession
from app.integrations.embeddings import EmbeddingClient, EmbeddingError
from app.integrations.hybrid_index import IndexNotReady
from app.integrations.knowledge_index import KnowledgeIndex
from app.models import KnowledgeChunk, KnowledgeDocument, Ticket
from app.modules.analysis.router import RetrievalConfig, get_embedding_client
from app.modules.knowledge.text import document_fingerprint
from app.modules.tickets.service import get_ticket

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/tickets", tags=["知识检索"])


class KnowledgeHit(BaseModel):
    document_id: int
    chunk_id: int
    title: str
    source_name: str
    chunk_order: int
    content: str
    fingerprint: str
    score: float
    scope: Literal["module", "category"]


class KnowledgeResult(BaseModel):
    items: list[KnowledgeHit]
    expanded: bool
    stale_count: int
    indexed_at: str


async def search_knowledge(
    ticket: Ticket, db: AsyncSession, config: RetrievalSettings, model: EmbeddingClient
) -> KnowledgeResult:
    query = f"标题：{ticket.title}\n问题：{ticket.description[:3000]}"
    async with await asyncio.to_thread(
        KnowledgeIndex,
        config.milvus_db_path,
        config.embedding_model,
        str(config.model_base_url),
        config.embedding_dimensions,
    ) as index:
        metadata = await asyncio.to_thread(index.ready)
        if metadata["count"] == 0:
            return KnowledgeResult(items=[], expanded=False, stale_count=0, indexed_at=metadata["built_at"])
        vector = (await asyncio.to_thread(model.embed, [query]))[0]
        items, seen = [], set()
        stale_count = 0
        expanded = False
        scopes: list[tuple[Literal["module", "category"], str]] = [
            ("module", f"product_id == {ticket.product_id} and module_id == {ticket.module_id}"),
            ("category", f"category_id == {ticket.category_id}"),
        ]
        for scope, expression in scopes:
            if scope == "category":
                expanded = True
            for hit in await asyncio.to_thread(index.search, query, vector, expression):
                chunk_id = int(hit["chunk_id"])
                if chunk_id in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(chunk_id)
                chunk = await db.get(KnowledgeChunk, chunk_id)
                if chunk is None:
                    stale_count += 1
                    continue
                doc = await db.get(KnowledgeDocument, chunk.document_id)
                if (
                    doc is None
                    or doc.status != "published"
                    or document_fingerprint(doc) != hit["entity"]["fingerprint"]
                ):
                    stale_count += 1
                    continue
                items.append(
                    KnowledgeHit(
                        document_id=doc.id,
                        chunk_id=chunk.id,
                        title=doc.title,
                        source_name=doc.source_name,
                        chunk_order=chunk.chunk_order,
                        content=chunk.content,
                        fingerprint=chunk.fingerprint,
                        score=hit["distance"],
                        scope=scope,
                    )
                )
                if len(items) == 5:
                    break
            if len(items) == 5:
                break
    return KnowledgeResult(items=items, expanded=expanded, stale_count=stale_count, indexed_at=metadata["built_at"])


@router.post("/{ticket_id}/knowledge", response_model=KnowledgeResult)
async def find_knowledge(
    ticket_id: int,
    db: DbSession,
    user: CurrentUser,
    config: RetrievalConfig,
    model: Annotated[EmbeddingClient, Depends(get_embedding_client)],
):
    ticket = await get_ticket(db, ticket_id)
    try:
        return await search_knowledge(ticket, db, config, model)
    except IndexNotReady as error:
        raise HTTPException(503, str(error)) from error
    except EmbeddingError as error:
        raise HTTPException(502, str(error)) from error
```

### 新增：backend/app/modules/knowledge/text.py

`backend/app/modules/knowledge/text.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 切分正文，并计算文档版本的内容指纹。

**固定写法：** hashlib.sha256是哈希函数。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 500是块长，420是移动距离，得到80重叠；改变它们后要重新发布和建索引。指纹用于发现过期引用。

<!-- source: 06-knowledge/backend/app/modules/knowledge/text.py -->
```python
import hashlib
import json

from app.models import KnowledgeDocument


def split_text(content: str) -> list[str]:
    """按字符切分，块长500、重叠80；仅在确有余文时继续。"""
    chunks = []
    start = 0
    while start < len(content):
        chunks.append(content[start : start + 500])
        if start + 500 >= len(content):
            break
        start += 420
    return chunks


def document_fingerprint(document: KnowledgeDocument) -> str:
    values = [
        document.title,
        document.content,
        document.product_id,
        document.module_id,
        document.category_id,
        document.revision,
    ]
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(values, ensure_ascii=False).encode()).hexdigest()
```

### 新增：backend/app/rebuild_knowledge.py

> **正式工程接线：** 下面的第六部分代码已对齐当前正式工程的 `create_app`、Settings 和异步会话工厂；保留现有登录保持、密码服务和中间件。


`backend/app/rebuild_knowledge.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 停后端后读取已发布分块，生成向量并建立完整知识索引。

**固定写法：** argparse、with、zip(strict=True)是标准或库写法，finish只在全部成功后执行。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** --replace允许替换知识派生索引；每批10条可按实际模型批量限制调整。这个命令保留案例集合和PostgreSQL。

<!-- source: 06-knowledge/backend/app/rebuild_knowledge.py -->
```python
import argparse
import asyncio

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import async_sessionmaker

from app.core.database import create_database_engine
from app.core.retrieval_settings import RetrievalSettings
from app.core.settings import Settings
from app.integrations.embeddings import EmbeddingClient, EmbeddingError
from app.integrations.knowledge_index import KnowledgeIndex
from app.models import KnowledgeChunk, KnowledgeDocument


async def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="停止后端后重建已发布知识索引")
    parser.add_argument("--replace", action="store_true", help="允许替换知识索引，保留业务表和案例索引")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    config = RetrievalSettings.model_validate({})
    engine = create_database_engine(Settings.load().database_url)
    session_factory = async_sessionmaker(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        async with session_factory() as db:
            async with await asyncio.to_thread(
                KnowledgeIndex,
                config.milvus_db_path,
                config.embedding_model,
                str(config.model_base_url),
                config.embedding_dimensions,
            ) as index:
                if await asyncio.to_thread(index.client.has_collection, "knowledge_chunks") and not args.replace:
                    raise SystemExit("知识索引已存在，请停后端后使用 --replace")
                rows = (
                    await db.execute(
                        select(KnowledgeChunk, KnowledgeDocument)
                        .join(KnowledgeDocument)
                        .where(KnowledgeDocument.status == "published")
                        .order_by(KnowledgeChunk.id)
                    )
                ).all()
                await asyncio.to_thread(index.reset)
                async with await asyncio.to_thread(
                    EmbeddingClient,
                    str(config.model_base_url),
                    config.model_api_key.get_secret_value(),
                    config.embedding_model,
                    config.embedding_dimensions,
                ) as model:
                    for start in range(0, len(rows), 10):
                        batch = rows[start : start + 10]
                        vectors = await asyncio.to_thread(
                            model.embed, [f"{doc.title}\n{chunk.content}" for chunk, doc in batch]
                        )
                        (
                            await asyncio.to_thread(
                                index.add,
                                [
                                    {
                                        "chunk_id": chunk.id,
                                        "content": f"{doc.title}\n{chunk.content}",
                                        "dense_vector": vector,
                                        "product_id": doc.product_id,
                                        "module_id": doc.module_id,
                                        "category_id": doc.category_id,
                                        "fingerprint": chunk.fingerprint,
                                    }
                                    for (chunk, doc), vector in zip(batch, vectors, strict=True)
                                ],
                            )
                        )
                        print(f"已索引 {start + len(batch)} / {len(rows)} 个知识分块", flush=True)
                await asyncio.to_thread(index.finish, len(rows))
                print("知识索引构建完成，可启动后端")
    except EmbeddingError as error:
        raise SystemExit(f"构建失败：{error}；知识索引未就绪") from None
    finally:
        await engine.dispose()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())
```

### 新增：backend/migrations/versions/06_knowledge.py

`backend/migrations/versions/06_knowledge.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 为已有数据库新增文档和分块两张表。

**固定写法：** upgrade、downgrade和op.create_table是Alembic约定。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** revision/down_revision连着旧迁移，表字段必须和models.py一致；不要随意改已有迁移历史。

<!-- source: 06-knowledge/backend/migrations/versions/06_knowledge.py -->
```python
"""知识文档与发布分块。"""

import sqlalchemy as sa
from alembic import op

revision = "06_knowledge"
down_revision = "04_import_key"
branch_labels = None
depends_on = None


def upgrade():
    op.create_table(
        "knowledge_documents",
        sa.Column("id", sa.Integer(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("title", sa.String(200), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("content", sa.Text(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("source_name", sa.String(200), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("product_id", sa.Integer(), sa.ForeignKey("products.id"), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("module_id", sa.Integer(), sa.ForeignKey("product_modules.id"), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("category_id", sa.Integer(), sa.ForeignKey("ticket_categories.id"), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("status", sa.String(20), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("revision", sa.Integer(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("chunk_count", sa.Integer(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("updated_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=sa.func.now(), nullable=False),
        sa.CheckConstraint("status IN ('draft','published','disabled')", name="knowledge_status_check"),
    )
    op.create_table(
        "knowledge_chunks",
        sa.Column("id", sa.Integer(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("document_id", sa.Integer(), sa.ForeignKey("knowledge_documents.id"), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("chunk_order", sa.Integer(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("content", sa.Text(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("fingerprint", sa.String(64), nullable=False),
        sa.UniqueConstraint("document_id", "chunk_order", name="knowledge_chunk_order_key"),
    )
    op.create_index("ix_knowledge_chunks_document_id", "knowledge_chunks", ["document_id"])


def downgrade():
    op.drop_table("knowledge_chunks")
    op.drop_table("knowledge_documents")
```

### 新增：backend/tests/test_knowledge.py

`backend/tests/test_knowledge.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 通过公开接口或真实外部边界检查行为，防止已有能力回退。

**固定写法：** pytest的test_命名、fixture、assert，以及HTTP MockTransport是测试写法。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 文案可以换；权限、状态码、真实引用和人工确认断言要遵循业务规则。模拟模型不等于真实模型效果已验证。

<!-- source: 06-knowledge/backend/tests/test_knowledge.py -->
```python
import pytest
from test_postgres_foundation import database as database
from test_tickets import ticket_context as ticket_context

from app.modules.knowledge.text import split_text


def test_split_text_keeps_overlap_and_tail():
    text = "".join(chr(0x4E00 + i) for i in range(1001))
    chunks = split_text(text)
    assert [len(chunk) for chunk in chunks] == [500, 500, 161]
    assert chunks[0][-80:] == chunks[1][:80]
    assert chunks[-1].endswith(text[-20:])


def document_body(catalog, **changes):
    return {
        **{key: catalog[key] for key in ("product_id", "module_id", "category_id")},
        "title": "E401 登录处理规范",
        "content": "# 登录规范\n重新绑定角色后验证登录。",
        **changes,
    }


@pytest.mark.postgres
async def test_knowledge_list_searches_title_and_content(ticket_context):
    client, headers, catalog = ticket_context
    for changes in [{}, {"title": "付款说明", "content": "重复扣款需核对流水号。"}]:
        response = await client.post("/api/knowledge", headers=headers, json=document_body(catalog, **changes))
        assert response.status_code == 201, response.text
    result = (await client.get("/api/knowledge?q=E401", headers=headers)).json()
    assert result["total"] == 1
    assert result["items"][0]["title"] == "E401 登录处理规范"
    result = (await client.get("/api/knowledge?q=流水号", headers=headers)).json()
    assert result["total"] == 1
    assert result["items"][0]["title"] == "付款说明"


@pytest.mark.postgres
async def test_admin_upload_edit_publish_disable(ticket_context):
    client, headers, catalog = ticket_context
    metadata = document_body(catalog)
    metadata.pop("content")
    response = await client.post(
        "/api/knowledge/upload",
        headers=headers,
        data=metadata,
        files={"file": ("login.md", "# E401\n重新绑定角色再登录。".encode(), "text/markdown")},
    )
    assert response.status_code == 201, response.text
    document = response.json()
    assert document["status"] == "draft"
    assert document["source_name"] == "login.md"
    path = f"/api/knowledge/{document['id']}"
    published = await client.post(path + "/publish", headers=headers)
    assert published.status_code == 200, published.text
    assert published.json()["chunk_count"] == 1
    assert (await client.post(path + "/publish", headers=headers)).status_code == 409
    updated = await client.put(
        path + "/content",
        headers=headers,
        json=document_body(catalog, title="新版登录规范", content="先检查租户和角色。"),
    )
    assert updated.status_code == 200
    assert updated.json()["status"] == "draft"
    assert updated.json()["chunk_count"] == 0
    assert updated.json()["revision"] > published.json()["revision"]
    assert (await client.post(path + "/publish", headers=headers)).status_code == 200
    assert (await client.post(path + "/disable", headers=headers)).json()["status"] == "disabled"
    assert (await client.get(path, headers=headers)).json()["content"] == "先检查租户和角色。"


@pytest.mark.postgres
async def test_staff_can_only_read_published_documents(ticket_context):
    client, headers, catalog = ticket_context
    credentials = {"username": "knowledge_staff", "password": "Only-test-12345", "display_name": "知识客服"}
    assert (await client.post("/api/users", headers=headers, json=credentials)).status_code == 201
    login = await client.post("/api/auth/login", json={key: credentials[key] for key in ("username", "password")})
    staff = {"Authorization": "Bearer " + login.json()["access_token"]}
    body = document_body(catalog)
    document = (await client.post("/api/knowledge", headers=headers, json=body)).json()
    path = f"/api/knowledge/{document['id']}"
    assert (await client.get("/api/knowledge")).status_code == 401
    assert (await client.get("/api/knowledge", headers=staff)).json()["total"] == 0
    assert (await client.get(path, headers=staff)).status_code == 403
    assert (await client.post("/api/knowledge", headers=staff, json=body)).status_code == 403
    assert (await client.put(path + "/content", headers=staff, json=body)).status_code == 403
    assert (await client.post(path + "/publish", headers=staff)).status_code == 403
    assert (await client.post(path + "/disable", headers=staff)).status_code == 403
    assert (await client.post(path + "/publish", headers=headers)).status_code == 200
    assert (await client.get("/api/knowledge", headers=staff)).json()["total"] == 1
    assert (await client.get(path, headers=staff)).json()["content"] == body["content"]
    assert (await client.post(path + "/disable", headers=headers)).status_code == 200
    assert (await client.get(path, headers=staff)).status_code == 403
    assert (await client.get("/api/knowledge", headers=staff)).json()["total"] == 0


@pytest.mark.postgres
@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("filename", "content", "status"),
    [
        ("image.png", b"not text", 422),
        ("text.md", b"\xff", 422),
        ("empty.txt", b"", 422),
        ("empty.md", b"   ", 422),
        ("large.txt", b"a" * (256 * 1024 + 1), 413),
    ],
)
async def test_invalid_upload_is_rejected(ticket_context, filename, content, status):
    client, headers, catalog = ticket_context
    metadata = document_body(catalog)
    metadata.pop("content")
    response = await client.post(
        "/api/knowledge/upload", headers=headers, data=metadata, files={"file": (filename, content)}
    )
    assert response.status_code == status, response.text
    assert (await client.get("/api/knowledge", headers=headers)).json()["total"] == 0


@pytest.mark.postgres
@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "changes",
    [
        {"title": " "},
        {"content": " "},
        {"content": "x" * 100001},
        {"module_id": 999999},
        {"status": "published"},
    ],
)
async def test_document_input_and_catalog_are_validated(ticket_context, changes):
    client, headers, catalog = ticket_context
    response = await client.post("/api/knowledge", headers=headers, json=document_body(catalog, **changes))
    assert response.status_code == 422, response.text
    assert (await client.get("/api/knowledge", headers=headers)).json()["total"] == 0
```

### 新增：backend/tests/test_knowledge_search.py

`backend/tests/test_knowledge_search.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 通过公开接口或真实外部边界检查行为，防止已有能力回退。

**固定写法：** pytest的test_命名、fixture、assert，以及HTTP MockTransport是测试写法。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 文案可以换；权限、状态码、真实引用和人工确认断言要遵循业务规则。模拟模型不等于真实模型效果已验证。

<!-- source: 06-knowledge/backend/tests/test_knowledge_search.py -->
```python
import asyncio

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select
from test_knowledge import document_body
from test_postgres_foundation import database as database
from test_similar import retrieval as retrieval

from app.integrations.knowledge_index import KnowledgeIndex
from app.models import KnowledgeChunk, KnowledgeDocument

pytestmark = pytest.mark.postgres


async def build_index(app, config):
    async with app.state.session_factory() as db:
        records = (
            await db.execute(
                select(KnowledgeChunk, KnowledgeDocument)
                .join(KnowledgeDocument)
                .where(KnowledgeDocument.status == "published")
            )
        ).all()
        rows = [
            {
                "chunk_id": chunk.id,
                "content": document.title + "\n" + chunk.content,
                "dense_vector": [1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0],
                "product_id": document.product_id,
                "module_id": document.module_id,
                "category_id": document.category_id,
                "fingerprint": chunk.fingerprint,
            }
            for chunk, document in records
        ]

    def build():
        with KnowledgeIndex(config.milvus_db_path, config.embedding_model, str(config.model_base_url), 4) as index:
            index.reset()
            if rows:
                index.add(rows)
            index.finish(len(rows))

    await asyncio.to_thread(build)


async def create_query(client, headers, catalog):
    response = await client.post(
        "/api/tickets",
        headers=headers,
        json={
            **catalog,
            "title": "E401 登录失败",
            "description": "角色绑定异常，无法登录。",
            "customer_name": "测试客户",
            "customer_email": "knowledge@example.com",
            "priority": "P2",
        },
    )
    assert response.status_code == 201, response.text
    return response.json()["id"]


async def test_retrieval_checks_publication_and_current_revision(retrieval):
    app, client, headers, catalog, config = retrieval
    document = (await client.post("/api/knowledge", headers=headers, json=document_body(catalog))).json()
    path = f"/api/knowledge/{document['id']}"
    assert (await client.post(path + "/publish", headers=headers)).status_code == 200
    ticket_id = await create_query(client, headers, catalog)
    query = f"/api/tickets/{ticket_id}/knowledge"
    await build_index(app, config)
    response = await client.post(query, headers=headers)
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    hit = response.json()["items"][0]
    assert hit["document_id"] == document["id"]
    assert hit["source_name"] == "手工录入"
    assert hit["chunk_order"] == 1
    assert hit["content"] == document["content"]
    assert (await client.post(query)).status_code == 401
    assert (await client.post(path + "/disable", headers=headers)).status_code == 200
    result = (await client.post(query, headers=headers)).json()
    assert result["items"] == []
    assert result["stale_count"] == 1
    assert (
        await client.put(
            path + "/content", headers=headers, json=document_body(catalog, content="新版：检查租户与账号角色。")
        )
    ).status_code == 200
    assert (await client.post(path + "/publish", headers=headers)).status_code == 200
    assert (await client.post(query, headers=headers)).json()["items"] == []
    await build_index(app, config)
    result = (await client.post(query, headers=headers)).json()
    assert result["items"][0]["content"] == "新版：检查租户与账号角色。"
    assert result["items"][0]["chunk_id"] != hit["chunk_id"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("same_module", [True, False])
async def test_search_limits_five_chunks_and_expands_category(retrieval, same_module):
    app, client, headers, catalog, config = retrieval
    body = document_body(catalog, content="E401 登录错误需要重新绑定角色。" * 160)
    if not same_module:
        module = (
            await client.post(
                "/api/config/modules", headers=headers, json={"name": "其他模块", "product_id": catalog["product_id"]}
            )
        ).json()
        body["module_id"] = module["id"]
    document = (await client.post("/api/knowledge", headers=headers, json=body)).json()
    assert (await client.post(f"/api/knowledge/{document['id']}/publish", headers=headers)).status_code == 200
    await build_index(app, config)
    ticket_id = await create_query(client, headers, catalog)
    response = await client.post(f"/api/tickets/{ticket_id}/knowledge", headers=headers)
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    result = response.json()
    assert len(result["items"]) == 5
    assert len({item["chunk_id"] for item in result["items"]}) == 5
    assert result["expanded"] is (not same_module)
    assert all(item["scope"] == ("module" if same_module else "category") for item in result["items"])


async def test_missing_knowledge_index_is_not_success(retrieval):
    app, client, headers, catalog, config = retrieval
    ticket_id = await create_query(client, headers, catalog)
    response = await client.post(f"/api/tickets/{ticket_id}/knowledge", headers=headers)
    assert response.status_code == 503
    assert "索引" in response.json()["detail"]
    assert (await client.post("/api/tickets/999999/knowledge", headers=headers)).status_code == 404
```
