`backend/tests/test_chat.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 通过公开接口或真实外部边界检查行为，防止已有能力回退。

**固定写法：** pytest的test_命名、fixture、assert，以及HTTP MockTransport是测试写法。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 文案可以换；权限、状态码、真实引用和人工确认断言要遵循业务规则。模拟模型不等于真实模型效果已验证。

<!-- source: 07-assistance/backend/tests/test_chat.py -->
```python
import json

import httpx2 as httpx
import pytest
from pydantic import BaseModel

from app.integrations.chat import ChatClient, ChatError


class Answer(BaseModel):
    answer: str


def test_chat_parses_json_and_disables_retries():
    def handler(request):
        assert b"json_object" in request.content
        assert "enable_thinking" not in json.loads(request.content)
        return httpx.Response(
            200,
            json={
                "id": "fixture",
                "object": "chat.completion",
                "created": 1,
                "model": "fixture",
                "choices": [
                    {
                        "index": 0,
                        "finish_reason": "stop",
                        "message": {"role": "assistant", "content": '{"answer":"检查角色"}'},
                    }
                ],
            },
        )

    with ChatClient(
        "https://model.example/v1", "fixture-key", "fixture", httpx.Client(transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
    ) as client:
        assert client.ask("返回JSON", {}, Answer).answer == "检查角色"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("content,finish", [("not json", "stop"), ("{}", "length")])
def test_chat_rejects_invalid_or_incomplete_response(content, finish):
    transport = httpx.MockTransport(
        lambda request: httpx.Response(
            200,
            json={
                "id": "fixture",
                "object": "chat.completion",
                "created": 1,
                "model": "fixture",
                "choices": [
                    {"index": 0, "finish_reason": finish, "message": {"role": "assistant", "content": content}}
                ],
            },
        )
    )
    with ChatClient("https://model.example/v1", "fixture-key", "fixture", httpx.Client(transport=transport)) as client:
        with pytest.raises(ChatError):
            client.ask("返回JSON", {}, Answer)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("status", [401, 429, 503])
def test_chat_preserves_http_failure_and_does_not_retry(status):
    calls = []

    def handler(request):
        calls.append(request)
        return httpx.Response(status, json={"error": {"message": "upstream failure"}})

    with ChatClient(
        "https://model.example/v1", "fixture-key", "fixture", httpx.Client(transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
    ) as client:
        with pytest.raises(ChatError, match=f"HTTP {status}"):
            client.ask("返回JSON", {}, Answer)
    assert len(calls) == 1
```

### 新增：frontend/src/api/assistance.ts

`frontend/src/api/assistance.ts`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 调用生成、分页历史和人工采用接口。

**固定写法：** Zod检查结构，request统一鉴权和错误。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 180秒是前端完整链路等待上限，不是性能目标；接口字段与后端一致。

<!-- source: 07-assistance/frontend/src/api/assistance.ts -->
```typescript
import { z } from 'zod'
import { request } from './http'

const stepSchema = z.object({ instruction: z.string(), source_ids: z.array(z.string()) })
const draftSchema = z.object({ summary: z.string(), diagnosis: z.string(), category_id: z.number(),
  priority: z.enum(['P1', 'P2', 'P3', 'P4']), reason: z.string(), steps: z.array(stepSchema),
  risks: z.array(z.string()), confirmations: z.array(z.string()), reply_subject: z.string(), reply_body: z.string() })
const sourceSchema = z.object({ source_id: z.string(), kind: z.enum(['ticket', 'knowledge']),
  entity_id: z.number(), title: z.string(), content: z.string(), fingerprint: z.string() })
export const assistanceSchema = z.object({ id: z.number(), ticket_id: z.number(), draft: draftSchema,
  sources: z.array(sourceSchema), model: z.string(), prompt_version: z.string(), duration_ms: z.number(),
  created_at: z.string(), adopted_at: z.string().nullable(), final_solution: z.string().nullable(), final_reply: z.string().nullable() })
export type Assistance = z.infer<typeof assistanceSchema>
export function generateAssistance(token: string, ticketId: number) {
  return request(`/tickets/${ticketId}/assist`, assistanceSchema, { token, method: 'POST', timeoutMs: 180000 })
}
export function listAssistance(token: string, ticketId: number, page: number) {
  return request(`/tickets/${ticketId}/assistance?page=${page}`, z.array(assistanceSchema), { token })
}
export function adoptAssistance(token: string, ticketId: number, runId: number, solution: string, reply: string) {
  return request(`/tickets/${ticketId}/assistance/${runId}/adopt`, assistanceSchema,
    { token, method: 'POST', body: { confirmed: true, solution, reply } })
}
```

### 新增：frontend/src/components/AssistancePanel.vue

`frontend/src/components/AssistancePanel.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 展示分析与引用，允许编辑方案和回复，勾选后保存采用，并读取历史。

**固定写法：** defineEmits向详情页通知刷新，v-model绑定编辑区。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 样式、文案可改；不要把生成按钮等同实际处理。确认和过期校验需要后端配合，不能删掉。

<!-- source: 07-assistance/frontend/src/components/AssistancePanel.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ElButton, ElCheckbox } from 'element-plus'
import 'element-plus/es/components/button/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/checkbox/style/css'
import { onMounted, onUnmounted, ref } from 'vue'
import { adoptAssistance, generateAssistance, listAssistance, type Assistance } from '../api/assistance'
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth'

const props = defineProps<{ ticketId: number; status: string; categories: Record<number, string> }>()
const emit = defineEmits<{ adopted: []; preview: [source: { kind: 'ticket' | 'knowledge'; id: number }] }>()
const auth = useAuthStore()
const current = ref<Assistance | null>(null)
const history = ref<Assistance[]>([])
const page = ref(1)
const solution = ref('')
const reply = ref('')
const confirmed = ref(false)
const busy = ref(false)
const generating = ref(false)
const elapsed = ref(0)
let timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | undefined
onUnmounted(() => clearInterval(timer))
const errorMessage = ref('')
function select(run: Assistance) {
  current.value = run
  solution.value = run.final_solution ?? run.draft.steps.map((step, i) => `${i + 1}. ${step.instruction} [${step.source_ids.join(', ')}]`).join('\n')
  reply.value = run.final_reply ?? `主题：${run.draft.reply_subject}\n\n${run.draft.reply_body}`
  confirmed.value = false
}
async function load() {
  history.value = await listAssistance(auth.token, props.ticketId, page.value)
}
onMounted(async () => {
  try {
    await load()
    if (history.value[0]) select(history.value[0])
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  }
})
async function perform(action: 'generate' | 'adopt' | 'history') {
  if (busy.value) return
  busy.value = true
  errorMessage.value = ''
  try {
    if (action === 'generate') {
      generating.value = true
      elapsed.value = 0
      const started = Date.now()
      timer = setInterval(() => { elapsed.value = Math.floor((Date.now() - started) / 1000) }, 1000)
      select(await generateAssistance(auth.token, props.ticketId))
      page.value = 1
      await load()
    } else if (action === 'adopt' && current.value) {
      select(await adoptAssistance(auth.token, props.ticketId, current.value.id, solution.value, reply.value))
      emit('adopted')
      await load()
    } else { await load() }
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  } finally { clearInterval(timer); generating.value = false; busy.value = false }
}
</script>

<template>
  <section class="panel assistance-panel">
    <div class="eyebrow">智能辅助 / 人工确认</div>
    <h2>AI 处理建议</h2>
    <p>基于真实检索依据生成，需人工核实；采用后保存处理记录和回复草稿。</p>
    <el-button type="primary" :disabled="busy || !['pending', 'processing'].includes(status)" @click="perform('generate')">生成处理建议</el-button>
    <p v-if="generating" role="status">正在生成，已等待 {{ elapsed }} 秒。{{ current ? '下方仍是上一次结果；完成后替换。' : '完成后显示新结果。' }}</p>
    <p v-else-if="busy" role="status">正在保存或读取记录……</p>
    <p v-if="errorMessage" role="alert" class="error">{{ errorMessage }}</p>
    <template v-if="current">
      <p class="muted">#{{ current.id }} · {{ current.model }} · {{ current.prompt_version }} · {{ current.duration_ms }} ms · {{ current.created_at }}</p>
      <h3>问题摘要与判断</h3>
      <p>{{ current.draft.summary }}</p><p>{{ current.draft.diagnosis }}</p>
      <p>建议分类：{{ categories[current.draft.category_id] }} · 建议优先级：{{ current.draft.priority }}</p>
      <p>{{ current.draft.reason }}</p>
      <ol><li v-for="(step, i) in current.draft.steps" :key="i">{{ step.instruction }} [{{ step.source_ids.join(', ') }}]</li></ol>
      <h3>风险</h3><p v-if="!current.draft.risks.length">模型未列出风险，请自行核实。</p>
      <ul><li v-for="item in current.draft.risks" :key="item">{{ item }}</li></ul>
      <h3>需要确认</h3><ul><li v-for="item in current.draft.confirmations" :key="item">{{ item }}</li></ul>
      <h3>参考来源快照</h3>
      <article v-for="source in current.sources" :key="source.source_id" class="assistance-source">
        <button class="text-button" type="button" @click="emit('preview', { kind: source.kind, id: source.entity_id })">[{{ source.source_id }}] {{ source.title }}</button>
        <p class="multiline">{{ source.content }}</p>
      </article>
      <h3>人工编辑并确认</h3>
      <p>以下编辑保存为采用记录；分类、优先级和已解决状态通过工单原有按钮确认。</p>
      <label class="field">处理方案<textarea v-model="solution" rows="6" maxlength="10000" style="width:100%" :disabled="busy || !!current.adopted_at" /></label>
      <label class="field">回复草稿<textarea v-model="reply" rows="6" maxlength="6000" style="width:100%" :disabled="busy || !!current.adopted_at" /></label>
      <p v-if="current.adopted_at">已于 {{ current.adopted_at }} 人工确认采用；邮件仍需在系统外发送。</p>
      <template v-else>
        <p><el-checkbox v-model="confirmed" :disabled="busy">我已核对工单、引用依据和待确认信息，确认保存采用记录</el-checkbox></p>
        <el-button type="primary" :disabled="busy || !confirmed || !solution.trim() || !reply.trim() || !['pending', 'processing'].includes(status)" @click="perform('adopt')">确认采用并保存记录</el-button>
      </template>
    </template>
    <h3>生成历史（每页10条）</h3>
    <p v-if="!history.length && !busy" class="muted">还没有生成记录。</p>
    <ul><li v-for="run in history" :key="run.id"><button class="text-button" type="button" :disabled="busy" @click="select(run)">#{{ run.id }} · {{ run.created_at }} · {{ run.adopted_at ? '已采用' : '未采用' }}</button></li></ul>
    <el-button :disabled="busy || page === 1" @click="page--; perform('history')">上一页</el-button>
    <el-button :disabled="busy || history.length < 10" @click="page++; perform('history')">下一页</el-button>
  </section>
</template>
```

### 修改：frontend/src/views/TicketDetailView.vue

`frontend/src/views/TicketDetailView.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 在原详情接入知识或建议组件，并在采用后重新读取处理记录。

**固定写法：** 组件属性传递和@adopted事件是Vue用法。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 组件位置可改；ticket.id必须来自当前工单。保留原开始、解决、关闭流程。

<!-- source: 07-assistance/frontend/src/views/TicketDetailView.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import AssistancePanel from '../components/AssistancePanel.vue'
import SourcePreview from '../components/SourcePreview.vue'
import SimilarCases from '../components/SimilarCases.vue'
import KnowledgeMatches from '../components/KnowledgeMatches.vue'
import { computed, onMounted, ref } from 'vue'
import { useRoute } from 'vue-router'
import AppIcon from '../components/AppIcon.vue'
import StatusBadge from '../components/StatusBadge.vue'
import { statusLabels, statusSchema } from '../api/models'
import type { TicketDetail, User } from '../api/models'
import { getCatalog } from '../api/config'
import type { Catalog } from '../api/config'
import { listUsers } from '../api/users'
import { getTicket, startTicket, addNote, resolveTicket, closeTicket } from '../api/tickets'
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth'
import { formatTime } from '../utils/format'

const route = useRoute()
const auth = useAuthStore()
const ticketId = Number(route.params.id)
const ticket = ref<TicketDetail | null>(null)
const catalog = ref<Catalog>({
  products: [],
  modules: [],
  categories: [],
})
const users = ref<User[]>([])
const statuses = statusSchema.options
const content = ref('')
const solution = ref('')
const resolveOpen = ref(false)
const replyConfirmed = ref(false)
const error = ref('')
const loading = ref(true)
const busy = ref(false)
const preview = ref<{ kind: 'ticket' | 'knowledge'; id: number } | null>(null)
const categoryNames = computed(() => Object.fromEntries(catalog.value.categories.map(item => [item.id, item.name])))

// computed 会随工单和基础资料变化重新计算，模板只负责显示结果。
const productName = computed(
  () => catalog.value.products.find((item) => item.id === ticket.value?.product_id)?.name,
)
const moduleName = computed(
  () => catalog.value.modules.find((item) => item.id === ticket.value?.module_id)?.name,
)
const categoryName = computed(
  () => catalog.value.categories.find((item) => item.id === ticket.value?.category_id)?.name,
)
const userNames = computed(() => new Map(users.value.map((user) => [user.id, user.display_name])))
const actionLabels = {
  pending: '开始处理',
  processing: '标记已解决',
  resolved: '关闭工单',
  closed: '已关闭',
}

onMounted(async () => {
  try {
    const [detail, config, members] = await Promise.all([
      getTicket(auth.token, ticketId),
      getCatalog(auth.token),
      listUsers(auth.token),
    ])
    catalog.value = config
    users.value = members
    ticket.value = detail
  } catch (caught) {
    if (!(caught instanceof Error)) throw caught
    error.value = caught.message
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
})
// 共用提交状态和错误提示；成功后用后端返回的完整工单更新页面。
async function perform(action: () => Promise<TicketDetail>): Promise<boolean> {
  if (busy.value) return false
  error.value = ''
  busy.value = true
  try {
    ticket.value = await action()
    return true
  } catch (caught) {
    if (!(caught instanceof Error)) throw caught
    error.value = caught.message
    return false
  } finally {
    busy.value = false
  }
}

async function addRecord() {
  if (await perform(() => addNote(auth.token, ticketId, content.value))) content.value = ''
}

async function primaryAction() {
  const status = ticket.value?.status
  if (status === 'processing') {
    resolveOpen.value = true
    return
  }
  if (status === 'pending') {
    await perform(() => startTicket(auth.token, ticketId))
    return
  }
  if (status === 'resolved' && replyConfirmed.value) {
    await perform(() => closeTicket(auth.token, ticketId))
  }
}

async function resolve() {
  if (await perform(() => resolveTicket(auth.token, ticketId, solution.value)))
    resolveOpen.value = false
}
</script>

<template>
  <template v-if="ticket">
    <div class="breadcrumb">
      <RouterLink to="/tickets">工单管理</RouterLink>
      <span>/</span>
      <span>{{ ticket.number }}</span>
    </div>
    <div class="detail-layout">
      <article class="ticket-detail">
        <div class="detail-top">
          <span class="ticket-code">{{ ticket.number }}</span>
          <RouterLink
            v-if="ticket.status !== 'closed'"
            :to="`/tickets/${ticket.id}/edit`"
            class="button outline"
          >
            编辑工单
          </RouterLink>
          <button
            :disabled="busy || (ticket.status === 'resolved' && !replyConfirmed)"
            v-if="ticket.status !== 'closed'"
            class="button primary"
            @click="primaryAction"
          >
            {{ actionLabels[ticket.status] }}
            <AppIcon name="arrow-right" :size="16" />
          </button>
          <span v-else class="closed-label">
            <AppIcon name="check" :size="17" />
            已归档，只读
          </span>
        </div>
        <h1 class="detail-title">{{ ticket.title }}</h1>
        <p class="detail-subtitle">
          创建于 {{ formatTime(ticket.created_at) }}
          <span>客户：{{ ticket.customer_name }} · {{ ticket.customer_email }}</span>
        </p>
        <div class="ticket-facts">
          <div>
            <small>当前状态</small>
            <StatusBadge :status="ticket.status" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <small>优先级</small>
            <span class="priority" :class="ticket.priority">
              <i />
              {{ ticket.priority }}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div>
            <small>所属产品</small>
            <span>{{ productName }}</span>
          </div>
          <div>
            <small>负责人</small>
            <span>{{ userNames.get(ticket.owner_id) }}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <p v-if="error && !resolveOpen" class="error error-panel" role="alert">
          {{ error }}
        </p>
        <label v-if="ticket.status === 'resolved'" class="confirm-reply">
          <input v-model="replyConfirmed" type="checkbox" />
          我已人工回复客户，确认关闭此工单
        </label>
        <section class="detail-section">
          <h2>问题描述</h2>
          <p class="multiline description">{{ ticket.description }}</p>
          <dl class="detail-meta">
            <dt>问题分类</dt>
            <dd>{{ categoryName }}</dd>
            <dt>所属模块</dt>
            <dd>{{ moduleName }}</dd>
          </dl>
        </section>
        <section v-if="ticket.solution" class="solution-box">
          <h2>
            <AppIcon name="check" :size="18" />
            解决方案
          </h2>
          <p class="multiline">{{ ticket.solution }}</p>
        </section>
        <section class="detail-section">
          <div class="section-title">
            <h2>处理记录</h2>
            <span>{{ ticket.records.length }} 条动态</span>
          </div>
          <ol class="timeline">
            <li v-for="record in ticket.records" :key="record.id">
              <span class="timeline-dot" />
              <span class="avatar">{{ '·' }}</span>
              <div>
                <div class="record-heading">
                  <strong>{{ userNames.get(record.author_id) }}</strong>
                  <time>{{ formatTime(record.created_at) }}</time>
                </div>
                <p class="multiline">{{ record.content }}</p>
              </div>
            </li>
          </ol>
        </section>
        <form v-if="ticket.status !== 'closed'" class="record-form" @submit.prevent="addRecord">
          <label for="record-content"><h2>添加处理记录</h2></label>
          <div class="composer">
            <textarea
              id="record-content"
              v-model="content"
              rows="3"
              maxlength="10000"
              placeholder="记录排查进展、沟通结果或下一步计划……"
              required
            />
            <footer>
              <span>记录将保存到数据库</span>
              <button type="submit" class="button primary" :disabled="busy || !content.trim()">
                添加记录
              </button>
            </footer>
          </div>
        </form>
        <p v-else class="readonly-note">此工单已关闭，问题描述、解决方案和处理记录均保留供查看。</p>
        <AssistancePanel :key="ticket.id" :ticket-id="ticket.id" :status="ticket.status"
          :categories="categoryNames" @adopted="perform(() => getTicket(auth.token, ticketId))"
          @preview="preview = $event" />
      </article>
      <aside class="detail-reference-stack">
        <SimilarCases :key="ticket.id" :ticket-id="ticket.id" />
        <KnowledgeMatches :key="ticket.id" :ticket-id="ticket.id" />
        <section class="reference-rail">
          <h2>处理指引</h2>
          <p class="rail-caption">按顺序完成处理，所有状态变化都留有记录。</p>
          <div class="workflow">
            <h3>处理流程</h3>
            <ol>
              <li
                v-for="(status, index) in statuses"
                :key="status"
                :class="{
                  done: statuses.indexOf(ticket.status) > index,
                  current: ticket.status === status,
                }"
              >
                <span>{{ statuses.indexOf(ticket.status) > index ? '✓' : index + 1 }}</span>
                {{ statusLabels[status] }}
              </li>
            </ol>
          </div>
          <p class="rail-footnote">
            工单与处理记录已保存到数据库。历史案例仅供参考，采用方案前请核对当前问题。
          </p>
        </section>
      </aside>
    </div>
    <SourcePreview v-if="preview" :key="`${preview.kind}-${preview.id}`" :kind="preview.kind" :id="preview.id" @close="preview = null" />
    <el-dialog
      v-model="resolveOpen"
      title="保存解决方案"
      width="min(560px, 92vw)"
      :close-on-click-modal="false"
    >
      <p class="muted">填写已采取的措施和处理结果，再将工单标记为已解决。</p>
      <label class="field" for="solution-content">
        <span>解决方案</span>
        <textarea
          id="solution-content"
          v-model="solution"
          rows="6"
          maxlength="10000"
          placeholder="说明问题原因、解决步骤和核对结果"
        />
      </label>
      <p v-if="error" role="alert" class="error">
        {{ error }}
      </p>
      <template #footer>
        <button class="button outline" @click="resolveOpen = false">取消</button>
        <button class="button primary" :disabled="busy || !solution.trim()" @click="resolve">
          保存并标记已解决
        </button>
      </template>
    </el-dialog>
  </template>
  <section v-else class="empty-state panel">
    <h1>{{ loading ? '正在读取工单……' : '无法加载工单' }}</h1>
    <p v-if="error" role="alert">
      {{ error }}
    </p>
    <RouterLink to="/tickets" class="button primary">返回工单列表</RouterLink>
  </section>
</template>
```

## 第八部分

### 修改：backend/app/integrations/hybrid_index.py

`backend/app/integrations/hybrid_index.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 案例和知识都需要建集合、存向量、判断就绪、加载和搜索，因此共享这些实际重复的逻辑。

**固定写法：** MilvusClient、add_field、load_collection、AnnSearchRequest、RRFRanker是库API。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 集合名、主键和元数据路径由两个具体索引传入；第八部分增加mode用于真实对比，默认仍是混合查询。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/backend/app/integrations/hybrid_index.py -->
```python
import asyncio
import json
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

from pymilvus import AnnSearchRequest, DataType, Function, FunctionType, MilvusClient, RRFRanker


class IndexNotReady(RuntimeError):
    """缺少完整索引，或当前配置与构建时不一致。"""


class HybridIndex:
    def __init__(
        self,
        path: Path,
        model: str,
        base_url: str,
        dimensions: int,
        collection: str,
        primary_key: str,
        metadata_path: Path,
    ):
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.collection = collection
        self.primary_key = primary_key
        self.metadata_path = metadata_path
        self.signature = {"model": model, "base_url": base_url.rstrip("/"), "dimensions": dimensions}
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.client = MilvusClient(str(path), timeout=10.0)

    async def __aenter__(self):
        return self

    async def __aexit__(self, exc_type, exc_value, traceback):
        await asyncio.to_thread(self.client.close)

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_value, traceback):
        self.client.close()

    def ready(self) -> dict:
        if not self.metadata_path.is_file():
            raise IndexNotReady("检索索引尚未完整建立，请运行重建命令")
        metadata = json.loads(self.metadata_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if any(metadata[key] != value for key, value in self.signature.items()):
            raise IndexNotReady("Embedding 模型、地址或维度已变更，请重建索引")
        if not self.client.has_collection(self.collection):
            raise IndexNotReady("索引元数据存在，但检索集合缺失，请重建索引")
        return metadata

    def reset(self) -> None:
        # 仅在后端停止后重建派生索引。先撤销就绪标记，失败时不能查询半套数据。
        self.metadata_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        if self.client.has_collection(self.collection):
            self.client.drop_collection(self.collection)
        schema = self.client.create_schema(auto_id=False, enable_dynamic_field=False)
        schema.add_field(self.primary_key, DataType.INT64, is_primary=True)
        schema.add_field(
            "content", DataType.VARCHAR, max_length=65535, enable_analyzer=True, analyzer_params={"tokenizer": "jieba"}
        )
        schema.add_field("dense_vector", DataType.FLOAT_VECTOR, dim=self.dimensions)
        schema.add_field("sparse_vector", DataType.SPARSE_FLOAT_VECTOR)
        for field in ["product_id", "module_id", "category_id"]:
            schema.add_field(field, DataType.INT64)
        schema.add_field("fingerprint", DataType.VARCHAR, max_length=64)
        schema.add_function(
            Function(
                name="content_bm25",
                function_type=FunctionType.BM25,
                input_field_names=["content"],
                output_field_names=["sparse_vector"],
            )
        )
        indexes = self.client.prepare_index_params()
        indexes.add_index("dense_vector", index_type="FLAT", metric_type="COSINE")
        indexes.add_index("sparse_vector", index_type="SPARSE_INVERTED_INDEX", metric_type="BM25")
        self.client.create_collection(self.collection, schema=schema, index_params=indexes)

    def add(self, rows: list[dict]) -> None:
        self.client.insert(self.collection, rows)

    def finish(self, count: int) -> None:
        self.client.flush(self.collection)
        metadata = {**self.signature, "count": count, "built_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()}
        temporary = self.metadata_path.with_suffix(".tmp")
        temporary.write_text(json.dumps(metadata, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
        temporary.replace(self.metadata_path)

    def search(
        self, text: str, vector: list[float], expression: str,
        mode: Literal["bm25", "dense", "hybrid"] = "hybrid",
    ) -> list[dict]:
        self.ready()
        # 索引由独立命令构建；新进程打开已有集合后，需要加载才能检索。
        self.client.load_collection(self.collection)
        if mode != "hybrid":
            field = "sparse_vector" if mode == "bm25" else "dense_vector"
            query = text if mode == "bm25" else vector
            metric = "BM25" if mode == "bm25" else "COSINE"
            return self.client.search(
                self.collection, data=[query], anns_field=field,
                search_params={"metric_type": metric}, filter=expression,
                limit=20, output_fields=["fingerprint"],
            )[0]
        requests = [
            AnnSearchRequest([vector], "dense_vector", {"metric_type": "COSINE"}, limit=20, expr=expression),
            AnnSearchRequest([text], "sparse_vector", {"metric_type": "BM25"}, limit=20, expr=expression),
        ]
        return self.client.hybrid_search(
            self.collection,
            reqs=requests,
            ranker=RRFRanker(60),
            limit=20,
            output_fields=["fingerprint"],
        )[0]
```

### 修改：backend/app/main.py

> **正式工程接线：** 本节完整参考使用教学工程的全局对象；当前正式工程使用 `create_app` / `app.state`。跟写本文件前先按[异步数据库与跟写约定](../../02-项目说明/异步数据库与跟写约定.md)选择该文件的替换代码，保留正式配置、密码服务和中间件，不能导入不存在的全局工厂。


`backend/app/main.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 把本部分的路由挂到已有FastAPI应用上。

**固定写法：** include_router是框架方法，异常处理和生命周期沿用已有实现。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 新增模块的导入和注册要对应；其他部分不用重写。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/backend/app/main.py -->
```python
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Awaitable, Callable
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from typing import cast
from uuid import uuid4

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, Response
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import async_sessionmaker

from app.core.database import create_database_engine
from app.core.errors import install_error_handlers
from app.core.passwords import PasswordService
from app.core.settings import Settings
from app.modules.analysis.assistance import router as assistance_router
from app.modules.analysis.evaluation import router as evaluation_router
from app.modules.analysis.router import router as analysis_router
from app.modules.config.router import router as config_router
from app.modules.knowledge.router import router as knowledge_router
from app.modules.knowledge.search import router as knowledge_search_router
from app.modules.tickets.import_csv import router as import_router
from app.modules.tickets.router import router as tickets_router
from app.modules.users.router import router as users_router


def create_app(settings: Settings | None = None) -> FastAPI:
    @asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(app: FastAPI) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
        config = settings if settings is not None else Settings.load()
        app.state.settings = config
        engine = create_database_engine(config.database_url)
        app.state.session_factory = async_sessionmaker(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
        app.state.passwords = PasswordService()
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            await engine.dispose()

    app = FastAPI(title="售后工单智能辅助系统", lifespan=lifespan)

    @app.middleware("http")
    async def request_id(request: Request, call_next: Callable[[Request], Awaitable[Response]]) -> Response:
        request.state.request_id = uuid4().hex
        response = await call_next(request)
        response.headers["X-Request-ID"] = cast(str, request.state.request_id)
        return response

    install_error_handlers(app)
    app.include_router(users_router)
    app.include_router(config_router)
    app.include_router(import_router)
    app.include_router(tickets_router)
    app.include_router(analysis_router)
    app.include_router(assistance_router)
    app.include_router(evaluation_router)
    app.include_router(knowledge_router)
    app.include_router(knowledge_search_router)

    @app.get("/api/health", tags=["健康检查"])
    async def health() -> dict[str, str]:
        return {"status": "ok"}

    return app


app = create_app()
```

### 新增：backend/app/modules/analysis/evaluation.py

`backend/app/modules/analysis/evaluation.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 提供不含密钥的索引模型、维度、构建时间及检索参数，写入评测记录。

**固定写法：** GET路由与鉴权依赖是框架接法。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 这里列的参数应与实际算法一致，修改算法时同步配置记录。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/backend/app/modules/analysis/evaluation.py -->
```python
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException

from app.core.security import CurrentUser
from app.integrations.case_index import CaseIndex
from app.integrations.hybrid_index import IndexNotReady
from app.integrations.knowledge_index import KnowledgeIndex
from app.modules.analysis.router import RetrievalConfig

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/evaluation", tags=["评测配置"])


@router.get("/config")
def evaluation_config(user: CurrentUser, config: RetrievalConfig):
    try:
        with CaseIndex(
            config.milvus_db_path, config.embedding_model, str(config.model_base_url), config.embedding_dimensions
        ) as cases:
            case_metadata = cases.ready()
        with KnowledgeIndex(
            config.milvus_db_path, config.embedding_model, str(config.model_base_url), config.embedding_dimensions
        ) as knowledge:
            knowledge_metadata = knowledge.ready()
    except IndexNotReady as error:
        raise HTTPException(503, str(error)) from error
    return {
        "cases": case_metadata,
        "knowledge": knowledge_metadata,
        "per_channel_candidates": 20,
        "rrf_k": 60,
        "max_results": 5,
        "scope_policy": "module_then_category",
        "chunk_size": 500,
        "chunk_overlap": 80,
    }
```

### 修改：backend/app/modules/analysis/router.py

`backend/app/modules/analysis/router.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 沿用案例检索，在第八部分增加三种模式以支持对比。

**固定写法：** Literal让FastAPI拒绝未知mode。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** mode只控制案例搜索，默认hybrid；单BM25不调用案例Embedding，知识和生成仍按各自流程。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/backend/app/modules/analysis/router.py -->
```python
import asyncio
from typing import Annotated, Literal

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, ValidationError

from app.core.retrieval_settings import RetrievalSettings
from app.core.security import CurrentUser, DbSession
from app.integrations.case_index import CaseIndex, IndexNotReady
from app.integrations.embeddings import EmbeddingClient, EmbeddingError
from app.models import Ticket
from app.modules.analysis.cases import case_fingerprint
from app.modules.tickets.service import get_ticket

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/tickets", tags=["相似案例"])


def get_retrieval_settings() -> RetrievalSettings:
    try:
        return RetrievalSettings.model_validate({})
    except ValidationError as error:
        fields = ", ".join(".".join(map(str, item["loc"])) for item in error.errors())
        raise HTTPException(503, f"检索配置缺失或不合法，请检查 backend/.env.retrieval：{fields}") from error


RetrievalConfig = Annotated[RetrievalSettings, Depends(get_retrieval_settings)]


def get_embedding_client(config: RetrievalConfig):
    with EmbeddingClient(
        str(config.model_base_url),
        config.model_api_key.get_secret_value(),
        config.embedding_model,
        config.embedding_dimensions,
    ) as model:
        yield model


class SimilarCase(BaseModel):
    id: int
    number: str
    title: str
    module_id: int
    solution: str
    score: float
    scope: Literal["module", "category"]


class SimilarResult(BaseModel):
    items: list[SimilarCase]
    expanded: bool
    stale_count: int
    indexed_at: str


@router.post("/{ticket_id}/similar", response_model=SimilarResult)
async def similar_cases(
    ticket_id: int,
    db: DbSession,
    user: CurrentUser,
    config: RetrievalConfig,
    model: Annotated[EmbeddingClient, Depends(get_embedding_client)],
    mode: Literal["bm25", "dense", "hybrid"] = "hybrid",
):
    ticket = await get_ticket(db, ticket_id)
    query = f"标题：{ticket.title}\n问题：{ticket.description[:3000]}"
    items = []
    seen = {ticket_id}
    stale_count = 0
    try:
        async with await asyncio.to_thread(
            CaseIndex,
            config.milvus_db_path,
            config.embedding_model,
            str(config.model_base_url),
            config.embedding_dimensions,
        ) as index:
            metadata = await asyncio.to_thread(index.ready)
            if metadata["count"] == 0:
                return SimilarResult(items=[], expanded=False, stale_count=0, indexed_at=metadata["built_at"])
            vector = [] if mode == "bm25" else (await asyncio.to_thread(model.embed, [query]))[0]
            scopes: list[tuple[Literal["module", "category"], str]] = [
                ("module", f"product_id == {ticket.product_id} and module_id == {ticket.module_id}"),
                ("category", f"category_id == {ticket.category_id}"),
            ]
            expanded = False
            for scope, expression in scopes:
                if scope == "category":
                    expanded = True
                expression += f" and ticket_id != {ticket_id}"
                for hit in await asyncio.to_thread(index.search, query, vector, expression, mode):
                    case_id = int(hit["ticket_id"])
                    if case_id in seen:
                        continue
                    seen.add(case_id)
                    case = await db.get(Ticket, case_id)
                    if (
                        case is None
                        or case.status not in {"resolved", "closed"}
                        or not case.solution.strip()
                        or case_fingerprint(case) != hit["entity"]["fingerprint"]
                    ):
                        stale_count += 1
                        continue
                    items.append(
                        SimilarCase(
                            id=case.id,
                            number=case.number,
                            title=case.title,
                            module_id=case.module_id,
                            solution=case.solution,
                            score=hit["distance"],
                            scope=scope,
                        )
                    )
                    if len(items) == 5:
                        break
                if len(items) == 5:
                    break
    except IndexNotReady as error:
        raise HTTPException(503, str(error)) from error
    except EmbeddingError as error:
        raise HTTPException(502, str(error)) from error
    return SimilarResult(items=items, expanded=expanded, stale_count=stale_count, indexed_at=metadata["built_at"])
```

```python
import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path

spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("evaluate", Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scripts/evaluate.py")
assert spec is not None and spec.loader is not None
evaluate = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(evaluate)


def test_recall_uses_all_relevant_cases_as_denominator():
    assert evaluate.recall_at_five([1, 2, 8, 9, 10, 3], [1, 2, 3, 4]) == 0.5


def test_macro_f1_includes_missed_class():
    assert evaluate.macro_f1([1, 1, 2, 2], [1, 1, 1, 1]) == 1 / 3
```

`backend/tests/test_retrieval_modes.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 通过公开接口或真实外部边界检查行为，防止已有能力回退。

**固定写法：** pytest的test_命名、fixture、assert，以及HTTP MockTransport是测试写法。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 文案可以换；权限、状态码、真实引用和人工确认断言要遵循业务规则。模拟模型不等于真实模型效果已验证。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/backend/tests/test_retrieval_modes.py -->
```python
import pytest
from test_import import csv_file, import_row
from test_knowledge_search import create_query
from test_postgres_foundation import database as database
from test_similar import build_index
from test_similar import retrieval as retrieval

from app.modules.analysis.router import get_embedding_client

pytestmark = pytest.mark.postgres


async def test_each_mode_and_bm25_without_embedding(retrieval):
    app, client, headers, catalog, config = retrieval
    row = await import_row(client, headers, catalog)
    ids = (await client.post("/api/tickets/import", headers=headers, files=csv_file([row]))).json()["ticket_ids"]
    await build_index(app, config, ids)
    ticket_id = await create_query(client, headers, catalog)
    for mode in ["dense", "hybrid"]:
        response = await client.post(f"/api/tickets/{ticket_id}/similar?mode={mode}", headers=headers)
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        assert response.json()["items"][0]["id"] == ids[0]

    class NoEmbedding:
        def embed(self, texts):
            raise AssertionError("BM25 must not call embedding")

    app.dependency_overrides[get_embedding_client] = lambda: NoEmbedding()
    response = await client.post(f"/api/tickets/{ticket_id}/similar?mode=bm25", headers=headers)
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    assert (await client.post(f"/api/tickets/{ticket_id}/similar?mode=unknown", headers=headers)).status_code == 422
```

### 新增：scripts/evaluate.py

`scripts/evaluate.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 读取独立标注工单，调用真实API，逐条记录并计算召回、分类、优先级和耗时。

**固定写法：** argparse、getpass、httpx和JSON读写是调用方式。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 数据路径、模式和输出文件名可改；先人工标注真实ID。--generate会产生远程费用和生成记录，不能把示例ID当实际样本。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/scripts/evaluate.py -->
```python
"""对已有独立评测工单调用真实API；从工程根目录运行。"""

import argparse
import getpass
import hashlib
import json
import statistics
import time
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

import httpx
from pydantic import (
    BaseModel,
    ConfigDict,
    Field,
    StrictInt,
    ValidationError,
    model_validator,
)


class Sample(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", hide_input_in_errors=True)
    sample_id: str = Field(min_length=1)
    ticket_id: StrictInt = Field(gt=0)
    relevant_case_ids: list[StrictInt] = Field(min_length=1)
    relevant_document_ids: list[StrictInt]
    expected_category_id: StrictInt = Field(gt=0)
    expected_priority: Literal["P1", "P2", "P3", "P4"]

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def unique_labels(self):
        if self.ticket_id in self.relevant_case_ids:
            raise ValueError("当前工单不得标成自身参考案例")
        for values in [self.relevant_case_ids, self.relevant_document_ids]:
            if len(values) != len(set(values)) or any(value <= 0 for value in values):
                raise ValueError("相关ID必须大于0且不能重复")
        return self


class LoginResponse(BaseModel):
    access_token: str = Field(min_length=1)


class CaseHit(BaseModel):
    id: StrictInt


class CaseResult(BaseModel):
    items: list[CaseHit] = Field(max_length=5)


class DocumentHit(BaseModel):
    document_id: StrictInt


class KnowledgeResult(BaseModel):
    items: list[DocumentHit] = Field(max_length=5)


class DraftResult(BaseModel):
    category_id: StrictInt
    priority: Literal["P1", "P2", "P3", "P4"]


class GenerationResult(BaseModel):
    id: StrictInt
    draft: DraftResult
    model: str
    prompt_version: str
    duration_ms: StrictInt


def recall_at_five(predicted: list[int], relevant: list[int]) -> float:
    return len(set(predicted[:5]) & set(relevant)) / len(set(relevant))


def macro_f1(expected: list[int], predicted: list[int]) -> float:
    scores = []
    for category in sorted(set(expected) | set(predicted)):
        true_positive = sum(
            a == category and b == category
            for a, b in zip(expected, predicted, strict=True)
        )
        false_positive = sum(
            a != category and b == category
            for a, b in zip(expected, predicted, strict=True)
        )
        false_negative = sum(
            a == category and b != category
            for a, b in zip(expected, predicted, strict=True)
        )
        scores.append(
            2 * true_positive / (2 * true_positive + false_positive + false_negative)
        )
    return statistics.mean(scores)


def load_samples(path: Path) -> list[Sample]:
    samples = [
        Sample.model_validate_json(line)
        for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
        if line.strip()
    ]
    if not samples:
        raise ValueError("评测集为空，请先人工标注")
    if len({sample.sample_id for sample in samples}) != len(samples) or len(
        {sample.ticket_id for sample in samples}
    ) != len(samples):
        raise ValueError("sample_id 与 ticket_id 均不得重复")
    return samples


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="独立评测；仅 --generate 会产生新的AI生成记录和模型费用"
    )
    parser.add_argument("--dataset", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--base-url", default="http://127.0.0.1:8000/api")
    parser.add_argument("--mode", choices=["bm25", "dense", "hybrid"], default="hybrid")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--generate",
        action="store_true",
        help="额外调用完整双源混合检索+重排+生成；不受--mode影响",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()
    parsed = urlsplit(args.base_url)
    if (
        parsed.scheme not in {"http", "https"}
        or not parsed.netloc
        or parsed.username
        or parsed.password
        or parsed.query
        or parsed.fragment
    ):
        raise SystemExit("base-url 必须是无账号、密码或查询参数的HTTP(S) API地址")
    try:
        samples = load_samples(args.dataset)
    except (ValidationError, ValueError) as error:
        raise SystemExit(f"评测集不合格：{error}") from None
    if args.output.exists():
        raise SystemExit("结果文件已存在，请使用新的文件名")
    username = input("登录用户名：").strip()
    password = getpass.getpass("登录密码（不回显）：")
    report = {
        "status": "running",
        "created_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
        "dataset_sha256": hashlib.sha256(args.dataset.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
        "case_mode": args.mode,
        "generate": args.generate,
        "generation_pipeline": "dual-source-hybrid-rerank-v1"
        if args.generate
        else None,
        "api_base_url": args.base_url,
        "details": [],
    }
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    # 每条完成即保存；中途失败会留下 failed 状态，不计算假成功的均值。
    with (
        args.output.open("x", encoding="utf-8") as output,
        httpx.Client(timeout=180.0) as client,
    ):

        def save_report():
            output.seek(0)
            json.dump(report, output, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            output.truncate()
            output.flush()

        def post(path, body=None):
            response = client.post(args.base_url.rstrip("/") + path, json=body)
            response.raise_for_status()
            return response.json()

        try:
            login = LoginResponse.model_validate(
                post("/auth/login", {"username": username, "password": password})
            )
            password = ""
            client.headers["Authorization"] = "Bearer " + login.access_token
            config = client.get(args.base_url.rstrip("/") + "/evaluation/config")
            config.raise_for_status()
            report["configuration"] = config.json()
            for sample in samples:
                started = time.monotonic()
                result = CaseResult.model_validate(
                    post(f"/tickets/{sample.ticket_id}/similar?mode={args.mode}")
                )
                knowledge = KnowledgeResult.model_validate(
                    post(f"/tickets/{sample.ticket_id}/knowledge")
                )
                predicted = [hit.id for hit in result.items]
                documents = list(
                    dict.fromkeys(hit.document_id for hit in knowledge.items)
                )
                item = {
                    "sample_id": sample.sample_id,
                    "ticket_id": sample.ticket_id,
                    "case_ids": predicted,
                    "document_ids": documents,
                    "case_recall_at_5": recall_at_five(
                        predicted, sample.relevant_case_ids
                    ),
                    "knowledge_recall_from_top5_chunks": recall_at_five(
                        documents, sample.relevant_document_ids
                    )
                    if sample.relevant_document_ids
                    else None,
                    "retrieval_pair_ms": round((time.monotonic() - started) * 1000),
                }
                if args.generate:
                    wall_started = time.monotonic()
                    generated = GenerationResult.model_validate(
                        post(f"/tickets/{sample.ticket_id}/assist")
                    )
                    item.update(
                        run_id=generated.id,
                        expected_category_id=sample.expected_category_id,
                        predicted_category_id=generated.draft.category_id,
                        expected_priority=sample.expected_priority,
                        predicted_priority=generated.draft.priority,
                        model=generated.model,
                        prompt_version=generated.prompt_version,
                        generation_server_ms=generated.duration_ms,
                        generation_wall_ms=round(
                            (time.monotonic() - wall_started) * 1000
                        ),
                    )
                report["details"].append(item)
                save_report()
                print(
                    f"已完成 {len(report['details'])}/{len(samples)}：{sample.sample_id}",
                    flush=True,
                )
        except (httpx.HTTPError, ValidationError, ValueError) as error:
            report["status"] = "failed"
            report["error"] = (
                f"HTTP {error.response.status_code}"
                if isinstance(error, httpx.HTTPStatusError)
                else type(error).__name__
            )
            save_report()
            raise SystemExit(
                f"评测中止，未汇总指标；检查后端与数据。记录：{args.output}"
            ) from None
        details = report["details"]
        labeled_knowledge = [
            item["knowledge_recall_from_top5_chunks"]
            for item in details
            if item["knowledge_recall_from_top5_chunks"] is not None
        ]
        report["metrics"] = {
            "count": len(details),
            "mean_case_recall_at_5": statistics.mean(
                item["case_recall_at_5"] for item in details
            ),
            "knowledge_labeled_count": len(labeled_knowledge),
            "mean_knowledge_recall_from_top5_chunks": statistics.mean(labeled_knowledge)
            if labeled_knowledge
            else None,
        }
        if args.generate:
            report["metrics"].update(
                category_macro_f1=macro_f1(
                    [item["expected_category_id"] for item in details],
                    [item["predicted_category_id"] for item in details],
                ),
                priority_accuracy=statistics.mean(
                    item["expected_priority"] == item["predicted_priority"]
                    for item in details
                ),
                mean_generation_wall_ms=statistics.mean(
                    item["generation_wall_ms"] for item in details
                ),
            )
        report["status"] = "completed"
        save_report()
    print(f"评测完成：{args.output}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### 新增：scripts/summarize_ratings.py

`scripts/summarize_ratings.py`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 校验人工评分CSV并计算各维度与总均值。

**固定写法：** csv.DictReader、statistics.mean、argparse是标准库接口。

**能改什么、要一起改什么：** 三项评分与1—5量表是当前规则，改后同时更新表头、验证和论文口径。空数据不能填默认分。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/scripts/summarize_ratings.py -->
```python
"""汇总人工评分；不代替人工评判。"""

import argparse
import csv
import json
import statistics
from pathlib import Path


def summarize(path: Path) -> dict:
    dimensions = ["relevance", "correctness", "usability"]
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as file:
        reader = csv.DictReader(file)
        if reader.fieldnames != ["sample_id", "run_id", "rater", *dimensions]:
            raise ValueError("评分表头与模板不一致")
        rows = list(reader)
    if not rows:
        raise ValueError("评分表为空；请人工填写后再运行")
    keys = set()
    for number, row in enumerate(rows, start=1):
        if None in row or any(value is None for value in row.values()):
            raise ValueError(f"第{number}条评分列数不正确")
        if (
            not row["sample_id"].strip()
            or not row["rater"].strip()
            or int(row["run_id"]) <= 0
        ):
            raise ValueError(f"第{number}条评分缺少样本、有效生成记录ID或评分人")
        key = (row["sample_id"], row["run_id"], row["rater"])
        if key in keys:
            raise ValueError(f"第{number}条为同一评分人的重复评分")
        keys.add(key)
        for field in dimensions:
            row[field] = int(row[field])
            if not 1 <= row[field] <= 5:
                raise ValueError(f"第{number}条的{field}必须为1—5整数")
    means = {field: statistics.mean(row[field] for row in rows) for field in dimensions}
    return {
        "ratings": len(rows),
        "samples": len({row["sample_id"] for row in rows}),
        "raters": len({row["rater"] for row in rows}),
        "means": means,
        "overall_mean": statistics.mean(means.values()),
    }


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="人工评分统计")
    parser.add_argument("--input", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    try:
        result = summarize(args.input)
    except ValueError as error:
        raise SystemExit(str(error)) from None
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with args.output.open("x", encoding="utf-8") as file:
        json.dump(result, file, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    print(f"已保存：{args.output}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```


## 最终工作台界面（完成第八部分后跟写）

先写工具和组件，再写页面、路由、App、main 和样式。下面文件都来自 `08-evaluation`；它们替换前面对应的旧版本。后端案例响应的 `module_id` 已同步到本页第八部分代码。

### App.vue

`frontend/src/App.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 决定显示登录页还是工作台，放入导航和当前业务页面。

**固定写法与能改的内容：** RouterView、computed 是 Vue 写法；页面路径与路由配置联动。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/App.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed } from 'vue'
import { useRoute } from 'vue-router'
import AppSidebar from './components/AppSidebar.vue'
import { useAuthStore } from './stores/auth'

const auth = useAuthStore()
const route = useRoute()

const isStandalone = computed(() => !auth.user || Boolean(route.meta.public))
const isTicketDetail = computed(() => /^\/tickets\/\d+$/.test(route.path))
</script>

<template>
  <div v-if="isStandalone" class="standalone">
    <RouterView />
  </div>
  <div v-else class="workbench">
    <AppSidebar />
    <main class="workbench-main">
      <div class="route-shell" :class="{ page: !isTicketDetail }">
        <RouterView v-slot="{ Component }">
          <KeepAlive include="TicketsView">
            <component :is="Component" :key="$route.path" />
          </KeepAlive>
        </RouterView>
      </div>
    </main>
  </div>
</template>
```

### main.ts

`frontend/src/main.ts`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 启动 Vue，注册路由、状态管理和组件库，启用深色主题。

**固定写法与能改的内容：** app.use 和 app.mount 是框架方法；主题引入要与 CSS 一起保留。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/main.ts -->
```ts
import { createApp } from 'vue'
import { createPinia } from 'pinia'
import { ElLoading } from 'element-plus'
import 'element-plus/es/components/loading/style/css'
import 'element-plus/theme-chalk/dark/css-vars.css'
import App from './App.vue'
import { router } from './router'
import './style.css'

document.documentElement.classList.add('dark')

const app = createApp(App)
app.use(createPinia())
app.use(router)
app.directive('loading', ElLoading.directive)
app.mount('#app')
```

### style.css

`frontend/src/style.css`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 集中定义深色三栏布局、按钮、时间线及窄屏显示。

**固定写法与能改的内容：** CSS 属性是浏览器语法；颜色、字号和间距可改，类名须和模板匹配。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/style.css -->
```css
/* 工作台主题：颜色取自设计图，避免各页面重复写色值。 */
:root {
  --bg: #151d29;
  --sidebar: #14253b;
  --sidebar-active: #213a5f;
  --rail: #22233b;
  --rail-border: #343a58;
  --line: #2d3a4d;
  --surface: #1e293b;
  --surface-deep: #151f2f;
  --text: #ffffff;
  --text-2: #d8e2f0;
  --muted: #a3b6d0;
  --muted-2: #7f8fa8;
  --accent: #86affd;
  --accent-ink: #16233b;
  --accent-soft: #bcd4ff;
  --p1: #f85743;
  --p2: #f6a13f;
  --p3: #61a7ff;
  --p4: #9aa9bd;
  --bar-off: #3a465c;
  --teal: #398084;
  --rail-text: #c8cbf5;
  --rail-muted: #a9aed0;
  --font: "Inter", "Segoe UI", "PingFang SC", "Microsoft YaHei", sans-serif;
}

* { box-sizing: border-box; }
html, body, #app { height: 100%; }
html { color-scheme: dark; }
body {
  margin: 0;
  background: var(--bg);
  color: var(--text);
  font-family: var(--font);
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  text-rendering: optimizeLegibility;
}
button, input, textarea, select { font-family: inherit; }
button { cursor: pointer; }
a { color: var(--accent); text-decoration: none; }
[hidden] { display: none !important; }
:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--accent); outline-offset: 2px; }

h1 { margin: 0 0 10px; font-size: 26px; line-height: 1.3; font-weight: 650; color: #f5f8fd; }
h2 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; line-height: 1.4; font-weight: 600; color: #f2f6fc; }
h3 { margin: 0; font-size: 15px; font-weight: 600; color: #e7eef9; }
p { line-height: 1.75; }
pre { white-space: pre-wrap; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.muted { color: var(--muted); font-size: 13px; }
.error { color: #ff9b8f; white-space: pre-wrap; }
.success { color: #8be3ad; }
.multiline { white-space: pre-wrap; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.el-select { width: 100%; }

/* 应用壳层 */
.standalone {
  min-height: 100vh;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  padding: 32px;
  background: var(--bg);
}
.workbench {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 240px minmax(0, 1fr);
  height: 100vh;
  overflow: hidden;
}
.workbench-main { min-width: 0; height: 100vh; overflow: hidden; }
.route-shell { height: 100%; }
.detail-skeleton { margin-top: 28px; }
.text-button { border: 0; background: transparent; padding: 0; font: inherit; color: var(--accent); text-align: left; }
.clamped { display: -webkit-box; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; -webkit-line-clamp: 4; overflow: hidden; }
.source-preview h3 { margin-top: 24px; }
.source-preview { overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.el-drawer { --el-drawer-bg-color: var(--bg); }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  *, *::before, *::after { animation-duration: .01ms !important; transition-duration: .01ms !important; }
}
.page {
  height: 100%;
  overflow-y: auto;
  padding: 36px 40px 56px;
  scrollbar-width: thin;
  scrollbar-color: #35435b transparent;
}

/* 左侧导航 */
.sidebar {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  min-height: 0;
  padding: 38px 12px 22px;
  background: var(--sidebar);
}
.brand { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; padding-left: 28px; }
.brand-mark { width: 34px; height: 34px; flex: 0 0 auto; }
.brand-name { font-size: 20px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .01em; line-height: 1.2; color: #f7faff; }
.brand-sub { margin-top: 4px; font-size: 11px; color: #94a6bd; letter-spacing: .02em; }

.nav { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; margin-top: 52px; }
.nav-item {
  position: relative;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 15px;
  width: 100%;
  height: 54px;
  padding: 0 16px 0 29px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 15px;
  text-align: left;
}
.nav-item svg { flex: 0 0 auto; }
.nav-item:hover:not(.active) { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, .035); color: #dbe4f0; }
.nav-item.active {
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(134, 175, 253, .17), rgba(134, 175, 253, .045));
  color: #dbe9ff;
}
.nav-item.active::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  left: 0;
  top: 15px;
  bottom: 15px;
  width: 3px;
  border-radius: 0 3px 3px 0;
  background: var(--accent);
}

.sidebar-user-wrap { position: relative; margin-top: auto; }
.sidebar-user {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  width: 100%;
  padding: 10px 8px 4px 28px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: transparent;

  text-align: left;
}
.sidebar-user:hover { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, .035); }
.avatar {
  display: grid;

}
.user-copy { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }
.user-name { font-size: 15px; color: #f4f7fc; line-height: 1.35; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.user-role { margin-top: 3px; font-size: 12px; color: #94a6bd; }
.user-caret { margin-left: auto; color: #8ea3c0; display: grid; place-items: center; }
.user-menu {
  position: absolute;
  left: 12px;
  right: 12px;
  bottom: 62px;
  z-index: 20;
  padding: 6px;
  border: 1px solid #33415a;
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: #1b2436;
  box-shadow: 0 18px 42px rgba(0, 0, 0, .38);
}
.user-menu button {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 9px;
  width: 100%;
  padding: 9px 10px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 7px;
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--text-2);
  font-size: 13px;
  text-align: left;
}
.user-menu button:hover { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, .06); color: #fff; }

/* 通用按钮 */
.btn {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 7px;
  height: 38px;
  min-width: 88px;
  padding: 0 18px;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-radius: 8px;
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 500;
  transition: background-color .15s ease, border-color .15s ease, opacity .15s ease;
}
.btn.primary { background: var(--accent); color: var(--accent-ink); min-width: 112px; font-weight: 600; }
.btn.primary:hover { background: #9cc0ff; }
.btn.ghost { background: transparent; border-color: #3a4356; color: #dbe4f0; }
.btn.ghost:hover { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, .04); border-color: #52627c; }
.btn.small { height: 34px; min-width: 0; padding: 0 14px; font-size: 13px; gap: 8px; }
.btn:disabled { opacity: .45; cursor: not-allowed; }
.btn.primary:disabled:hover { background: var(--accent); }

/* 状态与优先级 */
.status-badge {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  height: 30px;
  padding: 0 10px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 500;
}
.status-badge .dot { width: 9px; height: 9px; border-radius: 50%; }
.status--pending { background: #342b1d; color: #f6c15c; }
.status--pending .dot { background: #f9b30e; }
.status--processing { background: #1e2e4d; color: var(--accent-soft); }
.status--processing .dot { background: var(--accent); }
.status--resolved { background: #173225; color: #8be3ad; }
.status--resolved .dot { background: #4ade80; }
.status--closed { background: #262b36; color: #b3bcc9; }
.status--closed .dot { background: #94a3b8; }

.priority { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; }
.priority-bars { display: flex; gap: 3px; }
.priority-bars span { width: 14px; height: 12px; border-radius: 2px; background: var(--bar-off); }
.priority-bars--P1 span.on { background: var(--p1); }
.priority-bars--P2 span.on { background: var(--p2); }
.priority-bars--P3 span.on { background: var(--p3); }
.priority-bars--P4 span.on { background: var(--p4); }
.priority-label { font-size: 15px; color: #ff9d8d; }

/* 工单详情：主区 + 右侧参考栏 */
.ticket-detail {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) 388px;
  height: 100%;
}
.ticket-main {
  min-width: 0;
  height: 100%;
  overflow-y: auto;
  padding: 0 40px 54px 48px;
  scrollbar-width: thin;
  scrollbar-color: #35435b transparent;
}
.ticket-topbar {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  height: 92px;
  padding-top: 22px;
}
.breadcrumb { font-size: 14px; color: var(--muted-2); letter-spacing: .01em; }
.breadcrumb a { color: var(--muted); }
.breadcrumb a:hover { color: #dbe4f0; }
.breadcrumb .sep { margin: 0 9px; color: #4d5c73; }

.top-actions { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; }
.top-actions .btn { height: 40px; }
.page-alert { margin: 12px 0 0; }

.ticket-head { padding-top: 9px; }

  margin: 19px 0 0;

  line-height: 1.17;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: .005em;
}

.ticket-customer { margin-left: 18px; color: #7f8fa8; }

.meta {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 25.3% 23.5% 24.3% 26.9%;
  margin-top: 45px;
  padding-bottom: 37px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
}
.meta-item { min-width: 0; }
.meta-item + .meta-item { padding-left: 24px; border-left: 1px solid var(--line); }
.meta-label { display: block; margin-bottom: 7px; font-size: 12px; line-height: 16px; color: var(--muted-2); letter-spacing: .02em; }
.meta-value { display: flex; align-items: center; min-height: 30px; font-size: 15px; color: var(--text-2); }

.description {
  margin-top: 34px;
  padding-bottom: 32px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
}
.description p { margin: 24px 0 20px; font-size: 16px; line-height: 1.8; color: var(--text-2); }
.kv { display: flex; gap: 14px; margin-top: 6px; font-size: 14.5px; line-height: 1.7; }
.kv .k { width: 76px; flex: 0 0 auto; color: var(--muted); }

.solution-card {
  margin-top: 24px;

}

.solution-card p { margin: 10px 0 0; color: var(--text-2); font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.8; white-space: pre-wrap; }

.records-section { margin-top: 28px; }
.records-section > h2 { margin-bottom: 30px; }
.timeline { padding-bottom: 6px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }
.timeline-record {
  position: relative;

  grid-template-columns: 28px 38px minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
  column-gap: 18px;
  padding-bottom: 22px;
}
```
