```css
.timeline-dot {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
  width: 9px;
  height: 9px;
  margin: 8px 0 0 10px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--teal);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 4px rgba(57, 128, 132, .14);
}
.timeline-record::after {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  left: 14px;
  top: 20px;
  bottom: -1px;
  width: 1px;
  background: rgba(134, 175, 253, .18);
}
.timeline-record:last-child::after { display: none; }
.timeline-record--status .timeline-dot { background: var(--accent); box-shadow: 0 0 0 4px rgba(134, 175, 253, .13); }
.timeline-avatar {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 38px;
  height: 38px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--teal);
  color: #fff;
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 600;
}
.timeline-body { min-width: 0; padding-top: 6px; }
.timeline-name { font-size: 15px; color: #f4f7fc; line-height: 1.45; }
.timeline-text { margin-top: 9px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.75; color: var(--muted); overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.timeline-text strong { color: #dbe4f0; font-weight: 600; }
.timeline-text code {
  padding: 2px 5px;
  border-radius: 4px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, .06);
  color: #dbe4f0;
  font-family: ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Consolas, monospace;
  font-size: 13px;
}
.timeline-text a { color: var(--accent); }
.timeline-text ul { margin: 6px 0; padding-left: 20px; }
.timeline-time { padding-top: 4px; font-size: 13px; color: #8b9bb3; white-space: nowrap; }

.composer { margin-top: 21px; }
.composer > h2 { margin-bottom: 14px; }
.editor {
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: var(--surface);
  padding: 12px 18px;
}
.editor textarea {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 50px;
  padding: 0;
  border: 0;
  outline: 0;
  resize: vertical;
  background: transparent;
  color: #f5f8fd;
  font-size: 15px;
  line-height: 1.7;
}
.editor textarea::placeholder { color: #7f8fa8; }
.editor textarea:disabled { opacity: .6; cursor: not-allowed; }
.editor-bar { display: flex; align-items: flex-end; justify-content: space-between; gap: 16px; margin-top: 6px; }
.tools { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 14px; }
.tool {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 27px;
  height: 27px;
  padding: 0;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 5px;
  background: transparent;
  color: #9fb0c8;
  font-size: 16px;
  line-height: 1;
}
.tool:hover { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, .06); color: #fff; }
.tool:disabled { opacity: .4; cursor: not-allowed; }
.tool svg { width: 18px; height: 18px; }
.tool-bold { font-weight: 700; font-size: 17px; }
.tool-italic { font-style: italic; font-family: Georgia, serif; font-size: 18px; }
.closed-note { margin-top: 22px; padding: 14px 16px; border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 8px; color: var(--muted); font-size: 13px; }

/* 右侧参考栏 */
.reference-rail {
  min-height: 0;
  overflow-y: auto;
  padding: 89px 40px 48px 48px;
  background: var(--rail);
  border-left: 1px solid var(--rail-border);
  scrollbar-width: thin;
  scrollbar-color: #4a4d70 transparent;
}
.rail-section + .rail-section { margin-top: 120px; padding-top: 32px; border-top: 1px solid var(--rail-border); }
.rail-head { display: flex; align-items: flex-start; justify-content: space-between; gap: 16px; }
.rail-head h2 { font-size: 21px; color: #f5f3ff; letter-spacing: .01em; }
.rail-sub { margin: 14px 0 0; font-size: 13px; color: var(--rail-muted); }
.reference-rail .btn.ghost { border-color: #4d5074; color: var(--rail-text); min-width: 0; }
.reference-rail .btn.ghost:hover { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, .07); border-color: #656896; }
.rail-empty { margin: 30px 0 0; font-size: 13px; line-height: 1.8; color: var(--rail-muted); }
.rail-hint { margin: 18px 0 0; font-size: 12.5px; line-height: 1.7; color: var(--rail-muted); }
.rail-warning { margin: 12px 0 0; font-size: 12.5px; line-height: 1.7; color: #f6c15c; }
.rail-foot { margin: 43px 0 0; font-size: 13px; line-height: 1.65; color: var(--rail-muted); }

.case-card { margin-top: 48px; padding-bottom: 37px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--rail-border); }
.case-card + .case-card { margin-top: 30px; }
.case-number { font-size: 14px; color: var(--rail-muted); letter-spacing: .02em; }
.case-title { margin: 15px 0 11px; font-size: 17px; line-height: 1.5; font-weight: 600; color: #fff; }
.case-module { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; font-size: 13px; color: var(--rail-muted); }
.case-scope { padding: 1px 6px; border: 1px solid #45486a; border-radius: 4px; font-size: 11px; color: var(--rail-text); }
.case-label { margin-top: 27px; font-size: 14px; color: var(--rail-text); }
.case-solution { margin: 9px 0 0; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.8; color: var(--rail-text); }
.case-link { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-top: 37px; font-size: 14px; color: var(--rail-text); }
.case-link:hover { color: #fff; }

.doc-card { margin-top: 30px; padding-bottom: 26px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--rail-border); }
.doc-card:last-of-type { border-bottom: 0; }
.doc-title { font-size: 15px; font-weight: 600; color: #fff; line-height: 1.6; }
.doc-meta { margin-top: 6px; font-size: 12px; color: var(--rail-muted); }
.doc-text { margin: 10px 0 0; font-size: 13px; line-height: 1.8; color: var(--rail-text); white-space: pre-wrap; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }

/* 详情页内的旧面板（AI 建议等） */
.panel {
  background: #111a29;
  border: 1px solid #263349;
  border-radius: 12px;
  padding: 24px;
}
.assistance-panel { margin-top: 64px; }
.assistance-panel h2 { margin-bottom: 8px; }
.assistance-panel h3 { margin: 20px 0 8px; }
.assistance-panel ol, .assistance-panel ul { line-height: 1.8; color: var(--text-2); }
.assistance-panel label { display: block; margin-top: 12px; color: var(--muted); font-size: 13px; }
.assistance-panel textarea {
  margin-top: 6px;
  padding: 10px 12px;
  border: 1px solid #33415a;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: var(--surface-deep);
  color: var(--text-2);
  line-height: 1.7;
}
.record { border-left: 3px solid #35527c; padding: 8px 16px; margin: 16px 0; }

/* 列表、表单等旧页面 */
.page > h1 { margin-bottom: 18px; }
.panel + .panel { margin-top: 16px; }
.actions { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 14px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.filters { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr)); gap: 12px; margin: 20px 0; align-items: center; }
.form-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: 0 24px; }
.form-grid .full { grid-column: 1 / -1; }
.form-grid .actions { margin-top: 8px; }
.narrow { width: 100%; max-width: 460px; padding: 26px; }
.standalone .panel { box-shadow: 0 24px 70px rgba(0, 0, 0, .35); }

/* 原生表单控件（Element Plus 之外的少量页面使用） */
.panel input:not(.el-input__inner):not([type='checkbox']):not([type='radio']):not([type='file']),
.panel select,
.panel textarea:not(.el-textarea__inner) {
  padding: 8px 10px;
  border: 1px solid #33415a;
  border-radius: 6px;
  background: var(--surface-deep);
  color: var(--text-2);
}
.panel input:focus, .panel select:focus, .panel textarea:focus { outline: 1px solid var(--accent); }
.panel input[type='file'] { color: var(--muted); }

/* Element Plus 深色覆盖 */
html.dark {
  --el-color-primary: #86affd;
  --el-color-primary-light-3: #9cc0ff;
  --el-color-primary-light-5: #6f93d7;
  --el-color-primary-light-7: #3f567e;
  --el-color-primary-light-9: #1d2a42;
  --el-color-success: #4ade80;
  --el-color-warning: #f6c15c;
  --el-color-danger: #ff8f80;
  --el-bg-color: #151d29;
  --el-bg-color-page: #151d29;
  --el-bg-color-overlay: #1b2436;
  --el-fill-color-blank: #151f2f;
  --el-fill-color-light: #1e293b;
  --el-fill-color-lighter: #222f44;
  --el-fill-color: #1e293b;
  --el-border-color: #33415a;
  --el-border-color-light: #2d3a4d;
  --el-border-color-lighter: #2d3a4d;
  --el-text-color-primary: #e8eef9;
  --el-text-color-regular: #c8d5e6;
  --el-text-color-secondary: #a3b6d0;
  --el-text-color-placeholder: #7f8fa8;
  --el-text-color-disabled: #627089;
  --el-mask-color: rgba(7, 11, 18, .72);
  --el-box-shadow-light: 0 12px 34px rgba(0, 0, 0, .4);
  --el-border-radius-base: 8px;
}
html.dark .el-button--primary {
  --el-button-text-color: #16233b;
  --el-button-hover-text-color: #16233b;
  --el-button-active-text-color: #16233b;
  --el-button-disabled-text-color: #16233b;
}
html.dark .el-input__wrapper,
html.dark .el-select__wrapper,
html.dark .el-textarea__inner {
  background: var(--surface-deep);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px #33415a inset;
}
html.dark .el-input__wrapper.is-focus,
html.dark .el-select__wrapper.is-focused,
html.dark .el-textarea__inner:focus {
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px var(--accent) inset;
}
html.dark .el-table {
  --el-table-bg-color: transparent;
  --el-table-tr-bg-color: transparent;
  --el-table-header-bg-color: #1b2536;
  --el-table-border-color: #2d3a4d;
  --el-table-row-hover-bg-color: rgba(134, 175, 253, .08);
}
html.dark .el-dialog {
  background: #1b2436;
  border: 1px solid #33415a;
  border-radius: 14px;
}
html.dark .el-dialog__title { color: #f5f8fd; }
html.dark .el-dialog__body { color: var(--text-2); }
html.dark .el-checkbox__label { color: var(--text-2); }
html.dark .el-pagination { --el-pagination-bg-color: transparent; --el-pagination-text-color: var(--muted); }
html.dark .el-loading-mask { background-color: rgba(10, 16, 26, .7); }
html.dark .el-tag { --el-tag-bg-color: #1e293b; --el-tag-border-color: #33415a; --el-tag-text-color: #c8d5e6; }
html.dark .el-radio-button__inner { background: var(--surface-deep); border-color: #33415a; color: var(--muted); }
html.dark .el-radio-button__original-radio:checked + .el-radio-button__inner {
  background: #23375a;
  border-color: var(--accent);
  color: #dbe9ff;
  box-shadow: -1px 0 0 0 var(--accent);
}

/* 弹窗 */
.dialog-body { padding-top: 4px; }
.dialog-desc { margin: 0 0 12px; font-size: 13px; line-height: 1.75; color: var(--muted); }
.dialog-textarea {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 132px;
  padding: 12px 14px;
  border: 1px solid #33415a;
  border-radius: 8px;
  outline: 0;
  resize: vertical;
  background: var(--surface-deep);
  color: #f5f8fd;
  font-size: 14px;
  line-height: 1.7;
}
.dialog-textarea:focus { border-color: var(--accent); }
.dialog-textarea::placeholder { color: #73829a; }
.dialog-error { margin: 10px 0 0; font-size: 13px; color: #ff9b8f; }
.dialog-foot { display: flex; justify-content: flex-end; gap: 10px; padding-top: 18px; }
.check {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 14px;
  border: 1px solid #33415a;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: var(--surface-deep);
  font-size: 13.5px;
  line-height: 1.6;
  color: var(--text-2);
  cursor: pointer;
}
.check input { width: 16px; height: 16px; margin: 2px 0 0; accent-color: var(--accent); flex: 0 0 auto; }
.check strong { color: #fff; font-weight: 600; }

/* 响应式：窄屏时右栏下沉，不另做移动端布局 */
@media (max-width: 1180px) {
  .ticket-detail { display: block; height: 100%; overflow-y: auto; }
  .ticket-main { height: auto; overflow: visible; }
  .reference-rail { border-left: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--rail-border); padding: 40px 40px 48px 48px; overflow: visible; }
}
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .workbench { grid-template-columns: 72px minmax(0, 1fr); }
  .brand { padding-left: 13px; }
  .brand-name, .brand-sub, .nav-item span, .user-copy, .user-caret { display: none; }
  .nav-item { justify-content: center; padding: 0; }
  .sidebar-user { justify-content: center; padding: 10px 0 4px; }
  .user-menu { left: 8px; right: 8px; }
  .page { padding: 24px 20px 40px; }
  .ticket-main { padding: 0 20px 40px; }
  .reference-rail { padding: 32px 20px 40px; }
  .meta { grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; row-gap: 18px; }
  .meta-item:nth-child(3) { padding-left: 0; border-left: 0; }
  .meta-item:nth-child(4) { border-left: 1px solid var(--line); }
  .form-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
  .ticket-title { font-size: 30px; }
  .ticket-customer { display: block; margin: 6px 0 0; }
}
```

### router/index.ts

`frontend/src/router/index.ts`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 登记页面、限制管理员入口，并记住登录前要访问的地址。

**固定写法与能改的内容：** beforeEach 是路由守卫；redirect 是本项目登录返回参数，前后两处同时修改。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/router/index.ts -->
```ts
import { createRouter, createWebHistory } from 'vue-router'
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth'
const LoginView = () => import('../views/LoginView.vue')
const TicketsView = () => import('../views/TicketsView.vue')
const TicketEditor = () => import('../views/TicketEditor.vue')
const TicketDetailView = () => import('../views/TicketDetailView.vue')
const AdminView = () => import('../views/AdminView.vue')
const HealthView = () => import('../views/HealthView.vue')
const KnowledgeView = () => import('../views/KnowledgeView.vue')
const KnowledgeEditor = () => import('../views/KnowledgeEditor.vue')
const ImportView = () => import('../views/ImportView.vue')

export const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(),
  routes: [
    { path: '/', redirect: '/tickets' },
    { path: '/health', component: HealthView, meta: { public: true } },
    { path: '/login', component: LoginView, meta: { public: true } },
    { path: '/tickets', component: TicketsView },
    { path: '/tickets/new', component: TicketEditor },
    { path: '/tickets/import', component: ImportView, meta: { admin: true } },
    { path: '/tickets/:id', component: TicketDetailView },
    { path: '/tickets/:id/edit', component: TicketEditor },
    { path: '/knowledge', component: KnowledgeView },
    { path: '/knowledge/new', component: KnowledgeEditor, meta: { admin: true } },
    { path: '/knowledge/:id', component: KnowledgeEditor },
    { path: '/admin', component: AdminView, meta: { admin: true } },
  ],
})

router.beforeEach(async to => {
  const auth = useAuthStore()
  if (auth.token && !auth.user && to.path !== '/login') {
    try {
      await auth.restoreSession()
    } catch (error) {
      if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
      // 公开演示和连接检查不依赖登录，后端故障时仍允许访问。
      if (to.meta.public) return true
      return { path: '/login', query: { redirect: to.fullPath, session_error: `恢复登录失败：${error.message}` } }
    }
  }
  if (!to.meta.public && !auth.token) return { path: '/login', query: { redirect: to.fullPath } }
  if (to.meta.admin && auth.user?.role !== 'admin') return '/tickets'
})
```

### views/LoginView.vue

`frontend/src/views/LoginView.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 登录后返回原详情；直接登录时进入工单列表。

**固定写法与能改的内容：** useRoute 读取地址，router.replace 完成跳转；外部地址不能作为返回目标。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/views/LoginView.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ElButton, ElFormItem, ElInput } from 'element-plus'
import 'element-plus/es/components/button/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/form-item/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/input/style/css'
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth'

const auth = useAuthStore()
const router = useRouter()
const route = useRoute()
const route = useRoute()
const username = ref('')
const password = ref('')
const submitting = ref(false)
const errorMessage = ref(typeof route.query.session_error === 'string' ? route.query.session_error : '')

async function submit() {
  submitting.value = true
  errorMessage.value = ''
  try {
    await auth.signIn(username.value, password.value)
    password.value = ''
    // 新标签没有内存令牌；登录后回到原详情，只接受本应用已登记的非公开页面。
    const redirect = route.query.redirect
    let destination = '/tickets'
    if (typeof redirect === 'string' && redirect.startsWith('/') && !redirect.startsWith('//') && !redirect.includes('\\')) {
      const target = router.resolve(redirect)
      if (target.matched.length > 0 && !target.meta.public) destination = target.fullPath
    }
    await router.replace(destination)
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  } finally {
    submitting.value = false
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <section class="panel narrow">
    <h1>登录工作台</h1>
    <p class="muted">使用管理员创建的账号登录。</p>
    <p v-if="route.query.redirect" class="muted">此标签页需要单独登录，成功后返回刚才打开的页面。</p>
    <form @submit.prevent="submit">
      <el-form-item label="用户名">
        <el-input v-model="username" autocomplete="username" maxlength="50" />
      </el-form-item>
      <el-form-item label="密码">
        <el-input v-model="password" type="password" show-password autocomplete="current-password" />
      </el-form-item>
      <p v-if="errorMessage" role="alert" class="error">{{ errorMessage }}</p>
      <el-button type="primary" native-type="submit" :loading="submitting">登录</el-button>
    </form>
    <p class="muted">登录有效期为 8 小时，当前标签页刷新后可保持登录。</p>
  </section>
</template>
```

### views/TicketDetailView.vue

`frontend/src/views/TicketDetailView.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 展示工单并按状态提供开始处理、解决、关闭操作。

**固定写法与能改的内容：** 状态和接口是业务契约；标题文案可改，不能只改前端跳过后端状态流转。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/views/TicketDetailView.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import AssistancePanel from '../components/AssistancePanel.vue'
import SourcePreview from '../components/SourcePreview.vue'
import SimilarCases from '../components/SimilarCases.vue'
import KnowledgeMatches from '../components/KnowledgeMatches.vue'
import { computed, onMounted, ref } from 'vue'
import { useRoute } from 'vue-router'
import AppIcon from '../components/AppIcon.vue'
import StatusBadge from '../components/StatusBadge.vue'
import { statusLabels, statusSchema } from '../api/models'
import type { TicketDetail, User } from '../api/models'
import { getCatalog } from '../api/config'
import type { Catalog } from '../api/config'
import { listUsers } from '../api/users'
import { getTicket, startTicket, addNote, resolveTicket, closeTicket } from '../api/tickets'
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth'
import { formatTime } from '../utils/format'

const route = useRoute()
const auth = useAuthStore()
const ticketId = Number(route.params.id)
const ticket = ref<TicketDetail | null>(null)
const catalog = ref<Catalog>({
  products: [],
  modules: [],
  categories: [],
})
const users = ref<User[]>([])
const statuses = statusSchema.options
const content = ref('')
const solution = ref('')
const resolveOpen = ref(false)
const replyConfirmed = ref(false)
const error = ref('')
const loading = ref(true)
const busy = ref(false)
const preview = ref<{ kind: 'ticket' | 'knowledge'; id: number } | null>(null)
const categoryNames = computed(() => Object.fromEntries(catalog.value.categories.map(item => [item.id, item.name])))

// computed 会随工单和基础资料变化重新计算，模板只负责显示结果。
const productName = computed(
  () => catalog.value.products.find((item) => item.id === ticket.value?.product_id)?.name,
)
const moduleName = computed(
  () => catalog.value.modules.find((item) => item.id === ticket.value?.module_id)?.name,
)
const categoryName = computed(
  () => catalog.value.categories.find((item) => item.id === ticket.value?.category_id)?.name,
)
const userNames = computed(() => new Map(users.value.map((user) => [user.id, user.display_name])))
const actionLabels = {
  pending: '开始处理',
  processing: '标记已解决',
  resolved: '关闭工单',
  closed: '已关闭',
}

onMounted(async () => {
  try {
    const [detail, config, members] = await Promise.all([
      getTicket(auth.token, ticketId),
      getCatalog(auth.token),
      listUsers(auth.token),
    ])
    catalog.value = config
    users.value = members
    ticket.value = detail
  } catch (caught) {
    if (!(caught instanceof Error)) throw caught
    error.value = caught.message
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
})
// 共用提交状态和错误提示；成功后用后端返回的完整工单更新页面。
async function perform(action: () => Promise<TicketDetail>): Promise<boolean> {
  if (busy.value) return false
  error.value = ''
  busy.value = true
  try {
    ticket.value = await action()
    return true
  } catch (caught) {
    if (!(caught instanceof Error)) throw caught
    error.value = caught.message
    return false
  } finally {
    busy.value = false
  }
}

async function addRecord() {
  if (await perform(() => addNote(auth.token, ticketId, content.value))) content.value = ''
}

async function primaryAction() {
  const status = ticket.value?.status
  if (status === 'processing') {
    resolveOpen.value = true
    return
  }
  if (status === 'pending') {
    await perform(() => startTicket(auth.token, ticketId))
    return
  }
  if (status === 'resolved' && replyConfirmed.value) {
    await perform(() => closeTicket(auth.token, ticketId))
  }
}

async function resolve() {
  if (await perform(() => resolveTicket(auth.token, ticketId, solution.value)))
    resolveOpen.value = false
}
</script>

<template>
  <template v-if="ticket">
    <div class="breadcrumb">
      <RouterLink to="/tickets">工单管理</RouterLink>
      <span>/</span>
      <span>{{ ticket.number }}</span>
    </div>
    <div class="detail-layout">
      <article class="ticket-detail">
        <div class="detail-top">
          <span class="ticket-code">{{ ticket.number }}</span>
          <RouterLink
            v-if="ticket.status !== 'closed'"
            :to="`/tickets/${ticket.id}/edit`"
            class="button outline"
          >
            编辑工单
          </RouterLink>
          <button
            :disabled="busy || (ticket.status === 'resolved' && !replyConfirmed)"
            v-if="ticket.status !== 'closed'"
            class="button primary"
            @click="primaryAction"
          >
            {{ actionLabels[ticket.status] }}
            <AppIcon name="arrow-right" :size="16" />
          </button>
          <span v-else class="closed-label">
            <AppIcon name="check" :size="17" />
            已归档，只读
          </span>
        </div>
        <h1 class="detail-title">{{ ticket.title }}</h1>
        <p class="detail-subtitle">
          创建于 {{ formatTime(ticket.created_at) }}
          <span>客户：{{ ticket.customer_name }} · {{ ticket.customer_email }}</span>
        </p>
        <div class="ticket-facts">
          <div>
            <small>当前状态</small>
            <StatusBadge :status="ticket.status" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <small>优先级</small>
            <span class="priority" :class="ticket.priority">
              <i />
              {{ ticket.priority }}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div>
            <small>所属产品</small>
            <span>{{ productName }}</span>
          </div>
          <div>
            <small>负责人</small>
            <span>{{ userNames.get(ticket.owner_id) }}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <p v-if="error && !resolveOpen" class="error error-panel" role="alert">
          {{ error }}
        </p>
        <label v-if="ticket.status === 'resolved'" class="confirm-reply">
          <input v-model="replyConfirmed" type="checkbox" />
          我已人工回复客户，确认关闭此工单
        </label>
        <section class="detail-section">
          <h2>问题描述</h2>
          <p class="multiline description">{{ ticket.description }}</p>
          <dl class="detail-meta">
            <dt>问题分类</dt>
            <dd>{{ categoryName }}</dd>
            <dt>所属模块</dt>
            <dd>{{ moduleName }}</dd>
          </dl>
        </section>
        <section v-if="ticket.solution" class="solution-box">
          <h2>
            <AppIcon name="check" :size="18" />
            解决方案
          </h2>
          <p class="multiline">{{ ticket.solution }}</p>
        </section>
        <section class="detail-section">
          <div class="section-title">
            <h2>处理记录</h2>
            <span>{{ ticket.records.length }} 条动态</span>
          </div>
          <ol class="timeline">
            <li v-for="record in ticket.records" :key="record.id">
              <span class="timeline-dot" />
              <span class="avatar">{{ '·' }}</span>
              <div>
                <div class="record-heading">
                  <strong>{{ userNames.get(record.author_id) }}</strong>
                  <time>{{ formatTime(record.created_at) }}</time>
                </div>
                <p class="multiline">{{ record.content }}</p>
              </div>
            </li>
          </ol>
        </section>
        <form v-if="ticket.status !== 'closed'" class="record-form" @submit.prevent="addRecord">
          <label for="record-content"><h2>添加处理记录</h2></label>
          <div class="composer">
            <textarea
              id="record-content"
              v-model="content"
              rows="3"
              maxlength="10000"
              placeholder="记录排查进展、沟通结果或下一步计划……"
              required
            />
            <footer>
              <span>记录将保存到数据库</span>
              <button type="submit" class="button primary" :disabled="busy || !content.trim()">
                添加记录
              </button>
            </footer>
          </div>
        </form>
        <p v-else class="readonly-note">此工单已关闭，问题描述、解决方案和处理记录均保留供查看。</p>
        <AssistancePanel :key="ticket.id" :ticket-id="ticket.id" :status="ticket.status"
          :categories="categoryNames" @adopted="perform(() => getTicket(auth.token, ticketId))"
          @preview="preview = $event" />
      </article>
      <aside class="detail-reference-stack">
        <SimilarCases :key="ticket.id" :ticket-id="ticket.id" />
        <KnowledgeMatches :key="ticket.id" :ticket-id="ticket.id" />
        <section class="reference-rail">
          <h2>处理指引</h2>
          <p class="rail-caption">按顺序完成处理，所有状态变化都留有记录。</p>
          <div class="workflow">
            <h3>处理流程</h3>
            <ol>
              <li
                v-for="(status, index) in statuses"
                :key="status"
                :class="{
                  done: statuses.indexOf(ticket.status) > index,
                  current: ticket.status === status,
                }"
              >
                <span>{{ statuses.indexOf(ticket.status) > index ? '✓' : index + 1 }}</span>
                {{ statusLabels[status] }}
              </li>
            </ol>
          </div>
          <p class="rail-footnote">
            工单与处理记录已保存到数据库。历史案例仅供参考，采用方案前请核对当前问题。
          </p>
        </section>
      </aside>
    </div>
    <SourcePreview v-if="preview" :key="`${preview.kind}-${preview.id}`" :kind="preview.kind" :id="preview.id" @close="preview = null" />
    <el-dialog
      v-model="resolveOpen"
      title="保存解决方案"
      width="min(560px, 92vw)"
      :close-on-click-modal="false"
    >
      <p class="muted">填写已采取的措施和处理结果，再将工单标记为已解决。</p>
      <label class="field" for="solution-content">
        <span>解决方案</span>
        <textarea
          id="solution-content"
          v-model="solution"
          rows="6"
          maxlength="10000"
          placeholder="说明问题原因、解决步骤和核对结果"
        />
      </label>
      <p v-if="error" role="alert" class="error">
        {{ error }}
      </p>
      <template #footer>
        <button class="button outline" @click="resolveOpen = false">取消</button>
        <button class="button primary" :disabled="busy || !solution.trim()" @click="resolve">
          保存并标记已解决
        </button>
      </template>
    </el-dialog>
  </template>
  <section v-else class="empty-state panel">
    <h1>{{ loading ? '正在读取工单……' : '无法加载工单' }}</h1>
    <p v-if="error" role="alert">
      {{ error }}
    </p>
    <RouterLink to="/tickets" class="button primary">返回工单列表</RouterLink>
  </section>
</template>
```

### views/HealthView.vue

`frontend/src/views/HealthView.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 显示真实健康接口结果，复用深色页面容器。

**固定写法与能改的内容：** 请求及响应字段与后端保持一致；显示文案可以改。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/views/HealthView.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue'
import { getHealth } from '../api/health'
import type { HealthResponse } from '../api/health'

const isChecking = ref(false)
const healthStatus = ref<HealthResponse | null>(null)
const errorMessage = ref('')

async function checkHealth(): Promise<void> {
  isChecking.value = true
  healthStatus.value = null
  errorMessage.value = ''

  try {
    healthStatus.value = await getHealth()
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) {
      throw error
    }

    errorMessage.value = error.message
  } finally {
    isChecking.value = false
  }
}

onMounted(checkHealth)
</script>

<template>
  <main>
    <h1>售后工单智能辅助系统</h1>
    <p>检查前端与后端的接口连通性。</p>

    <p v-if="isChecking" role="status">正在检查……</p>
    <p v-else-if="errorMessage" role="alert" class="error">
      {{ errorMessage }}
    </p>
    <p v-else-if="healthStatus" role="status" class="success">
      前后端连接成功
    </p>

    <pre v-if="healthStatus">{{ JSON.stringify(healthStatus, null, 2) }}</pre>

    <button :disabled="isChecking" @click="checkHealth">
      {{ isChecking ? '检查中……' : '重新检查' }}
    </button>
  </main>
</template>

<style scoped>
main {
  max-width: 720px;
  margin: 80px auto;
  padding: 28px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: var(--surface);
  font-family: var(--font);
  color: var(--text-2);
}

.success {
  color: #8be3ad;
}

.error {
  color: #ff9b8f;
}

pre {
  padding: 16px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: var(--surface-deep);
  color: var(--text-2);
}

button {
  padding: 10px 18px;
  border: 1px solid #3a4356;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--text-2);
  cursor: pointer;
}

button:hover { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, .05); }
button:disabled { cursor: wait; opacity: .5; }
</style>
```

### api/analysis.ts

`frontend/src/api/analysis.ts`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 发送案例检索请求并检查响应，包含模块编号。

**固定写法与能改的内容：** Zod 的方法名固定；module_id 必须和后端响应同时存在。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/api/analysis.ts -->
```ts
import { z } from 'zod'
import { request } from './http'

const similarResult = z.object({
  items: z.array(z.object({
    id: z.number().int(), number: z.string(), title: z.string(), solution: z.string(),
    module_id: z.number().int(), score: z.number(), scope: z.enum(['module', 'category']),
  })),
  expanded: z.boolean(), stale_count: z.number().int(), indexed_at: z.string(),
})

export function findSimilarCases(token: string, ticketId: number) {
  return request(`/tickets/${ticketId}/similar`, similarResult, { token, method: 'POST', timeoutMs: 60000 })
}
```

### components/SimilarCases.vue

`frontend/src/components/SimilarCases.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 显示历史案例，在当前页面的只读抽屉查看原工单，保留当前草稿。

**固定写法与能改的内容：** preview 是子组件发给父组件的事件，父组件打开抽屉；事件名修改时两处要一起改。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/components/SimilarCases.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { findSimilarCases } from '../api/analysis'
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth'
import AppIcon from './AppIcon.vue'

const props = defineProps<{
  ticketId: number
  moduleNames: Record<number, string>
}>()
const emit = defineEmits<{ preview: [id: number] }>()
const auth = useAuthStore()
const result = ref<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof findSimilarCases>> | null>(null)
const busy = ref(false)
const expandedSolutions = ref(new Set<number>())
const errorMessage = ref('')

async function search() {
  busy.value = true
  errorMessage.value = ''
  try {
    result.value = await findSimilarCases(auth.token, props.ticketId)
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  } finally {
    busy.value = false
  }
}

function formatIndexedAt(value: string): string {
  return new Date(value).toLocaleString('zh-CN')
}
</script>

<template>
  <section class="rail-section">
    <div class="rail-head">
      <div>
        <h2>相似历史案例</h2>
        <p class="rail-sub">为当前问题提供历史参考</p>
      </div>
      <button class="btn ghost small" type="button" :disabled="busy" @click="search">
        <AppIcon name="search" :size="15" />
        {{ busy ? '搜索中…' : '搜索案例' }}
      </button>
    </div>

    <p v-if="errorMessage" class="error" role="alert">{{ errorMessage }}</p>
    <p v-if="busy" class="rail-empty" role="status">正在检索；已有卡片是上次结果。</p>

    <template v-if="result">
      <p v-if="result.items.length === 0" class="rail-empty">当前索引没有可返回的有效案例。</p>

      <article v-for="item in result.items" :key="item.id" class="case-card">
        <div class="case-number">{{ item.number }}</div>
        <h3 class="case-title">{{ item.title }}</h3>
        <div class="case-module">
          <span v-if="moduleNames[item.module_id]">{{ moduleNames[item.module_id] }}</span>
          <span v-if="item.scope === 'category'" class="case-scope">同分类补充</span>
        </div>
        <div class="case-label">解决方案：</div>
        <p class="case-solution" :class="{ 'clamped': item.solution.length > 120 && !expandedSolutions.has(item.id) }">{{ item.solution }}</p>
        <button v-if="item.solution.length > 120" class="text-button" type="button" @click="expandedSolutions.has(item.id) ? expandedSolutions.delete(item.id) : expandedSolutions.add(item.id)">{{ expandedSolutions.has(item.id) ? '收起方案' : '展开方案' }}</button>
        <button class="case-link text-button" type="button" @click="emit('preview', item.id)">
          查看原工单
          <AppIcon name="arrow-right" :size="15" />
        </button>
      </article>

      <p v-if="result.expanded" class="rail-hint">同产品模块的有效候选不足，已补查同分类案例。</p>
      <p v-if="result.stale_count > 0" class="rail-warning">已排除 {{ result.stale_count }} 条失效候选，请管理员重建案例索引。</p>

      <p class="rail-foot">
        索引时间：{{ formatIndexedAt(result.indexed_at) }}。结果按相关度排序，历史方案仅供参考，请结合当前问题核实。出处在当前页抽屉中预览，当前记录草稿会保留。
      </p>
    </template>

    <p v-else-if="!busy" class="rail-empty">点击“搜索案例”，按当前工单检索历史方案。</p>
  </section>
</template>
```

### components/KnowledgeMatches.vue

`frontend/src/components/KnowledgeMatches.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 展示知识片段及文档出处，在当前页面的只读抽屉查看文档。

**固定写法与能改的内容：** document_id 是完整文档编号，chunk_id 是片段编号，不能互换。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/components/KnowledgeMatches.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { searchKnowledge } from '../api/knowledge'
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth'
import AppIcon from './AppIcon.vue'

const props = defineProps<{ ticketId: number }>()
const emit = defineEmits<{ preview: [id: number] }>()
const auth = useAuthStore()
const result = ref<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof searchKnowledge>> | null>(null)
const busy = ref(false)
const errorMessage = ref('')

async function search() {
  errorMessage.value = ''
  busy.value = true
  try {
    result.value = await searchKnowledge(auth.token, props.ticketId)
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  } finally {
    busy.value = false
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <section class="rail-section">
    <div class="rail-head">
      <div>
        <h2>相关知识依据</h2>
        <p class="rail-sub">已发布的标准处理资料</p>
      </div>
      <button class="btn ghost small" type="button" :disabled="busy" @click="search">
        <AppIcon name="search" :size="15" />
        {{ busy ? '检索中…' : '检索知识' }}
      </button>
    </div>

    <p v-if="errorMessage" class="error" role="alert">{{ errorMessage }}</p>
    <p v-if="busy" class="rail-empty">正在检索；已有片段是上次结果。</p>

    <template v-if="result">
      <p class="rail-hint">
        {{ result.expanded ? '同产品模块候选不足，已补查同分类。' : '优先匹配同产品模块。' }}
      </p>
      <p v-if="result.stale_count > 0" class="rail-warning">发现 {{ result.stale_count }} 个失效候选，请管理员重建知识索引。</p>
      <p v-if="result.items.length === 0" class="rail-empty">本次没有找到可用知识依据。</p>

      <article v-for="item in result.items" :key="item.chunk_id" class="doc-card">
        <button class="doc-title text-button" type="button" @click="emit('preview', item.document_id)">
          {{ item.title }}
        </button>
        <div class="doc-meta">{{ item.source_name }} · 第 {{ item.chunk_order }} 块</div>
        <p class="doc-text">{{ item.content }}</p>
      </article>

      <p class="rail-foot">文档在当前页抽屉中预览，当前记录草稿会保留。检索结果仍需人工核实。</p>
    </template>

    <p v-else-if="!busy" class="rail-empty">点击“检索知识”，按当前工单查找已发布资料。</p>
  </section>
</template>
```

### components/AppSidebar.vue

`frontend/src/components/AppSidebar.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 显示导航、当前用户和退出入口。

**固定写法与能改的内容：** 管理员菜单由角色决定；后端权限检查仍须保留。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/components/AppSidebar.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, onMounted, onUnmounted, ref } from 'vue'
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth'
import AppIcon from './AppIcon.vue'

type NavItem = {
  to: string
  label: string
  icon: 'ticket' | 'clock' | 'settings' | 'book'
}

const auth = useAuthStore()
const route = useRoute()
const router = useRouter()
const menuOpen = ref(false)
const userWrap = ref<HTMLElement | null>(null)

const navItems = computed<NavItem[]>(() => {
  const items: NavItem[] = [{ to: '/tickets', label: '工单管理', icon: 'ticket' }]
  if (auth.user?.role === 'admin') {
    items.push(
      { to: '/tickets/import', label: '历史导入', icon: 'clock' },
      { to: '/admin', label: '基础管理', icon: 'settings' },
    )
  }
  items.push({ to: '/knowledge', label: '知识库', icon: 'book' })
  return items
})

const roleLabel = computed(() => auth.user?.role === 'admin' ? '管理员' : '售后客服')

function isActive(to: string): boolean {
  if (to === '/tickets') {
    return route.path.startsWith('/tickets') && route.path !== '/tickets/import'
  }
  return route.path === to || route.path.startsWith(to + '/')
}

function signOut() {
  auth.signOut()
  void router.push('/login')
}

function onDocumentClick(event: MouseEvent) {
  if (!menuOpen.value) return
  const target = event.target as Node
  if (!userWrap.value?.contains(target)) menuOpen.value = false
}

onMounted(() => document.addEventListener('click', onDocumentClick))
onUnmounted(() => document.removeEventListener('click', onDocumentClick))
</script>

<template>
  <aside class="sidebar">
    <div class="brand">
      <svg class="brand-mark" viewBox="0 0 34 34" aria-hidden="true">
        <rect x="2.5" y="8" width="13" height="13" rx="2.2" fill="#86affd" opacity=".92" />
        <rect x="14" y="2.5" width="13" height="13" rx="2.2" fill="#dbe7ff" opacity=".42" />
        <rect x="17.5" y="17.5" width="12" height="12" rx="2.2" fill="#86affd" opacity=".55" />
      </svg>
      <div>
        <div class="brand-name">售后工单</div>
        <div class="brand-sub">客户支持工作台</div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <nav class="nav" aria-label="主导航">
      <RouterLink
        v-for="item in navItems"
        :key="item.to"
        :to="item.to"
        class="nav-item"
        :class="{ active: isActive(item.to) }"
      >
        <AppIcon :name="item.icon" :size="19" />
        <span>{{ item.label }}</span>
      </RouterLink>
    </nav>

    <div ref="userWrap" class="sidebar-user-wrap">
      <button class="sidebar-user" type="button" :aria-expanded="menuOpen" @click="menuOpen = !menuOpen">
        <span class="avatar">{{ auth.user?.display_name.slice(0, 1) ?? '?' }}</span>
        <span class="user-copy">
          <span class="user-name">{{ auth.user?.display_name ?? '未登录' }}</span>
          <span class="user-role">{{ roleLabel }}</span>
        </span>
        <span class="user-caret"><AppIcon name="chevron-down" :size="16" /></span>
      </button>
      <div v-if="menuOpen" class="user-menu">
        <button type="button" @click="signOut">
          <AppIcon name="logout" :size="16" />
          退出登录
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </aside>
</template>
```

### components/AppIcon.vue

`frontend/src/components/AppIcon.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 绘制工作台使用的 SVG 图标。

**固定写法与能改的内容：** SVG 元素是浏览器语法；图形可以改，图标名字和调用处同步。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/components/AppIcon.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
type IconName =
  | 'ticket'
  | 'clock'
  | 'settings'
  | 'book'
  | 'search'
  | 'arrow-right'
  | 'info'
  | 'chevron-down'
  | 'pencil'
  | 'logout'

defineProps<{ name: IconName; size?: number }>()
</script>

<template>
  <svg
    :width="size ?? 20"
    :height="size ?? 20"
    viewBox="0 0 24 24"
    fill="none"
    stroke="currentColor"
    stroke-width="1.7"
    stroke-linecap="round"
    stroke-linejoin="round"
```

### components/TicketTimeline.vue

`frontend/src/components/TicketTimeline.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 显示处理人、内容和记录时间。

**固定写法与能改的内容：** v-for 遍历记录；只渲染经过 renderNote 处理的内容。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/components/TicketTimeline.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import type { TicketDetail } from '../api/models'
import { renderNote } from '../utils/markdown'
import { formatRecordTime } from '../utils/time'

const props = defineProps<{
  records: TicketDetail['records']
  userNames: Record<number, string>
}>()

function authorName(authorId: number): string {
  return props.userNames[authorId] ?? `用户 #${authorId}`
}

function initial(authorId: number): string {
  const name = props.userNames[authorId]
  return name ? name.slice(0, 1) : '#'
}
</script>

<template>
  <div class="timeline">
    <article
      v-for="record in records"
      :key="record.id"
      class="timeline-record"
      :class="{ 'timeline-record--status': record.kind !== 'note' }"
    >
      <span class="timeline-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span>
      <span class="timeline-avatar" aria-hidden="true">{{ initial(record.author_id) }}</span>
      <div class="timeline-body">
        <div class="timeline-name">{{ authorName(record.author_id) }}</div>
        <div class="timeline-text" v-html="renderNote(record.content)"></div>
      </div>
      <time class="timeline-time" :datetime="record.created_at">{{ formatRecordTime(record.created_at) }}</time>
    </article>
    <p v-if="records.length === 0" class="muted">暂无处理记录。</p>
  </div>
</template>
```

### components/NoteComposer.vue

`frontend/src/components/NoteComposer.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 输入记录并插入加粗、链接等 Markdown 标记。

**固定写法与能改的内容：** emit 向父组件传递内容和提交事件；没有附件接口，不提供附件按钮。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/components/NoteComposer.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, nextTick, ref } from 'vue'

const props = defineProps<{
  modelValue: string
  busy: boolean
}>()
const emit = defineEmits<{
  'update:modelValue': [value: string]
  submit: []
}>()

const textarea = ref<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>(null)
const canSubmit = computed(() => props.modelValue.trim().length > 0 && !props.busy)

function setValue(value: string) {
  emit('update:modelValue', value)
}

function insertAround(before: string, after: string, placeholder: string) {
  const element = textarea.value
  if (!element) return
  const start = element.selectionStart
  const end = element.selectionEnd
  const selected = props.modelValue.slice(start, end) || placeholder
  setValue(props.modelValue.slice(0, start) + before + selected + after + props.modelValue.slice(end))
  void nextTick(() => {
    element.focus()
    element.setSelectionRange(start + before.length, start + before.length + selected.length)
  })
}

function insertList() {
  const element = textarea.value
  if (!element) return
  const start = element.selectionStart
  const lineStart = props.modelValue.lastIndexOf('\n', start - 1) + 1
  setValue(props.modelValue.slice(0, lineStart) + '- ' + props.modelValue.slice(lineStart))
  void nextTick(() => {
    element.focus()
    element.setSelectionRange(start + 2, start + 2)
  })
}

function submit() {
  if (!canSubmit.value) return
  emit('submit')
}
</script>

<template>
  <section class="composer">
    <h2>添加处理记录</h2>
    <div class="editor">
      <textarea
        ref="textarea"
        :value="modelValue"
        rows="2"
        maxlength="10000"
        placeholder="记录排查过程或解决方案..."
        :disabled="busy"
        @input="setValue(($event.target as HTMLTextAreaElement).value)"
        @keydown.ctrl.enter.prevent="submit"
        @keydown.meta.enter.prevent="submit"
      ></textarea>
      <div class="editor-bar">
        <div class="tools" aria-label="格式工具">
          <button class="tool tool-bold" type="button" title="加粗" :disabled="busy" @click="insertAround('**', '**', '加粗文字')">B</button>
          <button class="tool tool-italic" type="button" title="斜体" :disabled="busy" @click="insertAround('*', '*', '斜体文字')">I</button>
          <button class="tool" type="button" title="插入链接" aria-label="插入链接" :disabled="busy" @click="insertAround('[', '](https://)', '链接文字')">
            <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
              <path d="M10 13.8a4.2 4.2 0 0 0 6 0l2.2-2.2a4.2 4.2 0 0 0-6-6l-1.2 1.2" />
              <path d="M14 10.2a4.2 4.2 0 0 0-6 0l-2.2 2.2a4.2 4.2 0 0 0 6 6l1.2-1.2" />
            </svg>
          </button>
          <button class="tool" type="button" title="列表" aria-label="列表" :disabled="busy" @click="insertList">
            <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
              <path d="M9 6h11M9 12h11M9 18h11" />
              <path d="M4.5 6h.01M4.5 12h.01M4.5 18h.01" />
            </svg>
          </button>
          <button class="tool" type="button" title="行内代码" aria-label="行内代码" :disabled="busy" @click="insertAround('`', '`', '代码')">
            <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
              <path d="m8 8-4 4 4 4M16 8l4 4-4 4" />
            </svg>
          </button>
        </div>
        <button class="btn primary" type="button" :disabled="!canSubmit" @click="submit">
          {{ busy ? '提交中…' : '添加记录' }}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>
</template>
```

### utils/time.ts

`frontend/src/utils/time.ts`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 统一格式化创建时间与处理时间。

**固定写法与能改的内容：** 日期 API 固定；显示格式可按业务修改。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/utils/time.ts -->
```ts
const pad = (value: number) => String(value).padStart(2, '0')

export function formatCreatedAt(value: string): string {
  const date = new Date(value)
  const now = new Date()
  const datePart = date.getFullYear() === now.getFullYear()
    ? `${date.getMonth() + 1}月${date.getDate()}日`
    : `${date.getFullYear()}年${date.getMonth() + 1}月${date.getDate()}日`
  return `${datePart} ${pad(date.getHours())}:${pad(date.getMinutes())}`
}

export function formatRecordTime(value: string): string {
  const date = new Date(value)
  const now = new Date()
  const time = `${pad(date.getHours())}:${pad(date.getMinutes())}`
  if (date.toDateString() === now.toDateString()) return time
  if (date.getFullYear() === now.getFullYear()) {
    return `${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())} ${time}`
  }
  return `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())} ${time}`
}
```

### utils/markdown.ts

`frontend/src/utils/markdown.ts`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

**它做什么：** 转义用户文字，再渲染有限的 Markdown 格式。

**固定写法与能改的内容：** 转义和链接协议限制是安全边界；不要直接将原文交给 v-html。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/utils/markdown.ts -->
```ts
const escapeMap: Record<string, string> = {
  '&': '&amp;',
  '<': '&lt;',
  '>': '&gt;',
  '"': '&quot;',
  "'": '&#39;',
}

// 处理记录以 Markdown 文本保存；先转义再生成少量固定标签，避免 v-html 注入。
export function renderNote(text: string): string {
  let html = text.replace(/[&<>"']/g, char => escapeMap[char] ?? char)
  const codes: string[] = []
  html = html.replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, (_, code: string) => {
    codes.push(code)
    return `\u0000${codes.length - 1}\u0000`
  })
  html = html
    .replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>')
    .replace(/\*([^*]+)\*/g, '<em>$1</em>')
    .replace(
      /\[([^\]]+)\]\((https?:\/\/[^)\s]+)\)/g,
      '<a href="$2" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">$1</a>',
    )
    .replace(/\u0000(\d+)\u0000/g, (_, index: string) => `<code>${codes[Number(index)]}</code>`)
  html = html.replace(/(^|\n)((?:- [^\n]*(?:\n|$))+)/g, (_, prefix: string, block: string) => {
    const items = block.trimEnd().split('\n').map(line => `<li>${line.replace(/^- /, '')}</li>`).join('')
    return `${prefix}<ul>${items}</ul>\n`
  })
  return html.replaceAll('\n', '<br>')
}
```

### scripts/check_navigation.mjs

**用途：** 复查未登录跳转和登录后返回，运行真实路由守卫及登录提交代码；不连接真实账号。

**固定与可改：** Node assert 用于断言；用例中的工单编号可改。脚本复用前端现有 TypeScript、Vue Router 依赖，不需要安装测试框架。

`scripts/check_navigation.mjs`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/scripts/check_navigation.mjs -->
```js
import assert from 'node:assert/strict'
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
const { fileURLToPath } = await import('node:url')
const root = fileURLToPath(new URL('../frontend/', import.meta.url)).replace(/\/$/, '')
const source = readFileSync(`${root}/src/router/index.ts`, 'utf8')
let guard
new Function('router', 'useAuthStore', source.slice(source.indexOf('router.beforeEach')))(
  { beforeEach: callback => { guard = callback } },
  () => ({ token: '', user: null }),
)
assert.deepEqual(guard({ meta: {}, fullPath: '/tickets/26' }), { path: '/login', query: { redirect: '/tickets/26' } })
assert.deepEqual(guard({ meta: {}, fullPath: '/knowledge/3' }), { path: '/login', query: { redirect: '/knowledge/3' } })
console.log('目标详情在未登录跳转时保留：通过')
const { createRequire } = await import('node:module')
const require = createRequire(`${root}/package.json`)
const ts = require('typescript')
const { createRouter, createMemoryHistory } = require('vue-router')
const { ref } = require('vue')
const loginSource = readFileSync(`${root}/src/views/LoginView.vue`, 'utf8').match(/<script setup lang="ts">([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/)[1]
const compiled = ts.transpileModule(loginSource.replace(/^import .*$/gm, '') + '\nreturn { submit }', { compilerOptions: { target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2022 } }).outputText
for (const [redirect, expected] of [
  ['/tickets/26', '/tickets/26'], ['/knowledge/3', '/knowledge/3'],
  [undefined, '/tickets'], ['https://outside.example', '/tickets'],
  ['//outside.example', '/tickets'], ['/login', '/tickets'],
  [['/tickets/26'], '/tickets'],
]) {
  const router = createRouter({ history: createMemoryHistory(), routes: [
    { path: '/tickets', component: {} }, { path: '/tickets/:id', component: {} },
    { path: '/knowledge/:id', component: {} }, { path: '/login', component: {}, meta: { public: true } },
  ] })
  const { submit } = new Function('ref', 'useRouter', 'useRoute', 'useAuthStore', compiled)(
    ref, () => router, () => ({ query: { redirect } }), () => ({ signIn: async () => {} }),
  )
  await submit()
  assert.equal(router.currentRoute.value.fullPath, expected)
}
console.log('真实登录提交逻辑：目标工单、知识、直接登录及非法返回地址共7例通过')
```

### api/assistance.ts

`frontend/src/api/assistance.ts`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

组件的导入名称及样式路径按 Element Plus 规定填写；页面标题和提示文案可改，接口字段和事件名称要与调用方一致。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/api/assistance.ts -->
```typescript
import { z } from 'zod'
import { request } from './http'

const stepSchema = z.object({ instruction: z.string(), source_ids: z.array(z.string()) })
const draftSchema = z.object({ summary: z.string(), diagnosis: z.string(), category_id: z.number(),
  priority: z.enum(['P1', 'P2', 'P3', 'P4']), reason: z.string(), steps: z.array(stepSchema),
  risks: z.array(z.string()), confirmations: z.array(z.string()), reply_subject: z.string(), reply_body: z.string() })
const sourceSchema = z.object({ source_id: z.string(), kind: z.enum(['ticket', 'knowledge']),
  entity_id: z.number(), title: z.string(), content: z.string(), fingerprint: z.string() })
export const assistanceSchema = z.object({ id: z.number(), ticket_id: z.number(), draft: draftSchema,
  sources: z.array(sourceSchema), model: z.string(), prompt_version: z.string(), duration_ms: z.number(),
  created_at: z.string(), adopted_at: z.string().nullable(), final_solution: z.string().nullable(), final_reply: z.string().nullable() })
export type Assistance = z.infer<typeof assistanceSchema>
export function generateAssistance(token: string, ticketId: number) {
  return request(`/tickets/${ticketId}/assist`, assistanceSchema, { token, method: 'POST', timeoutMs: 180000 })
}
export function listAssistance(token: string, ticketId: number, page: number) {
  return request(`/tickets/${ticketId}/assistance?page=${page}`, z.array(assistanceSchema), { token })
}
export function adoptAssistance(token: string, ticketId: number, runId: number, solution: string, reply: string) {
  return request(`/tickets/${ticketId}/assistance/${runId}/adopt`, assistanceSchema,
    { token, method: 'POST', body: { confirmed: true, solution, reply } })
}
```

### api/config.ts

`frontend/src/api/config.ts`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

组件的导入名称及样式路径按 Element Plus 规定填写；页面标题和提示文案可改，接口字段和事件名称要与调用方一致。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/api/config.ts -->
```typescript
import { z } from 'zod'
import { request } from './http'
import { moduleSchema, namedSchema } from './models'

export type ConfigKind = 'products' | 'modules' | 'categories'

export async function getCatalog(token: string) {
  const [products, modules, categories] = await Promise.all([
    request('/config/products', z.array(namedSchema), { token }),
    request('/config/modules', z.array(moduleSchema), { token }),
    request('/config/categories', z.array(namedSchema), { token }),
  ])
  return { products, modules, categories }
}

export function createConfig(token: string, kind: ConfigKind, name: string, product_id?: number) {
  if (kind === 'modules') {
    return request('/config/modules', moduleSchema, {
      token, method: 'POST', body: { name, product_id },
    })
  }
  return request(`/config/${kind}`, namedSchema, { token, method: 'POST', body: { name } })
}

export function updateConfig(token: string, kind: ConfigKind, id: number, name: string, is_active: boolean) {
  return request(`/config/${kind}/${id}`, namedSchema, {
    token, method: 'PUT', body: { name, is_active },
  })
}
```

### api/health.ts

`frontend/src/api/health.ts`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

组件的导入名称及样式路径按 Element Plus 规定填写；页面标题和提示文案可改，接口字段和事件名称要与调用方一致。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/api/health.ts -->
```typescript
export interface HealthResponse {
  status: 'ok'
}

export async function getHealth(): Promise<HealthResponse> {
  const response = await fetch('/api/health', {
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000),
  })

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`健康检查请求失败：HTTP ${response.status}`)
  }

  const data: unknown = await response.json()

  if (
    typeof data !== 'object' ||
    data === null ||
    !('status' in data) ||
    data.status !== 'ok'
  ) {
    throw new Error('健康检查响应不符合约定，应返回 {"status":"ok"}')
  }

  return { status: data.status }
}
```

### api/http.ts

`frontend/src/api/http.ts`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

组件的导入名称及样式路径按 Element Plus 规定填写；页面标题和提示文案可改，接口字段和事件名称要与调用方一致。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/api/http.ts -->
```typescript
import { z } from 'zod'

const errorSchema = z.object({
  detail: z.union([
    z.string(),
    z.array(z.object({
      loc: z.array(z.union([z.string(), z.number()])),
      msg: z.string(),
    })),
  ]),
})

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(public readonly status: number, message: string) {
    super(message)
    this.name = 'ApiError'
  }
}

interface RequestOptions {
  token?: string
  method?: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'PATCH'
  body?: unknown
  timeoutMs?: number
}

// 登录、配置、工单都需要 JSON 请求、错误展示和超时，因此在此复用。
export async function request<T>(
  path: string, schema: z.ZodType<T>, options: RequestOptions = {},
): Promise<T> {
  const headers = new Headers()
  if (options.token) headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${options.token}`)
  const isUpload = options.body instanceof FormData
  if (options.body !== undefined && !isUpload) headers.set('Content-Type', 'application/json')
  let body: BodyInit | undefined
  if (options.body instanceof FormData) body = options.body
  else if (options.body !== undefined) body = JSON.stringify(options.body)

  const response = await fetch('/api' + path, {
    method: options.method ?? 'GET',
    headers,
    body,
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(options.timeoutMs ?? 10000),
  })

  if (!response.ok) {
    let message = `请求失败：HTTP ${response.status}`
    // FastAPI 的业务/校验错误是 JSON；未处理的服务端异常可能只有文本。
    if (response.headers.get('content-type')?.includes('application/json')) {
      const { detail } = errorSchema.parse(await response.json())
      message = typeof detail === 'string'
        ? detail
        : detail.map(issue => `${issue.loc.join('.')}: ${issue.msg}`).join('；')
    }
    throw new ApiError(response.status, message)
  }

  return schema.parse(await response.json())
}
```

```typescript
import { z } from 'zod'
import { request } from './http'

const importResult = z.object({ imported: z.number().int(), ticket_ids: z.array(z.number().int()) })

export function importTickets(token: string, file: File) {
```

### api/knowledge.ts

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/api/knowledge.ts -->
```typescript
import { z } from 'zod'
import { request } from './http'

export const documentSchema = z.object({
  id: z.number(), title: z.string(), content: z.string(), source_name: z.string(),
  product_id: z.number(), module_id: z.number(), category_id: z.number(),
  status: z.enum(['draft', 'published', 'disabled']), revision: z.number(),
  chunk_count: z.number(), updated_at: z.string(),
})
export type KnowledgeDocument = z.infer<typeof documentSchema>
export type DocumentInput = Pick<KnowledgeDocument, 'title' | 'content' | 'product_id' | 'module_id' | 'category_id'>
const pageSchema = z.object({ items: z.array(documentSchema), total: z.number(), page: z.number() })
const hitSchema = z.object({ document_id: z.number(), chunk_id: z.number(), title: z.string(),
  source_name: z.string(), chunk_order: z.number(), content: z.string(), fingerprint: z.string(),
  score: z.number(), scope: z.enum(['module', 'category']) })
export const knowledgeResultSchema = z.object({ items: z.array(hitSchema), expanded: z.boolean(),
  stale_count: z.number(), indexed_at: z.string() })

export function listKnowledge(token: string, page: number) {
  return request(`/knowledge?page=${page}`, pageSchema, { token })
}
export function getKnowledge(token: string, id: number) {
  return request(`/knowledge/${id}`, documentSchema, { token })
}
export function saveKnowledge(token: string, body: DocumentInput, id?: number) {
  return request(id ? `/knowledge/${id}/content` : '/knowledge', documentSchema,
    { token, method: id ? 'PUT' : 'POST', body })
}
export function uploadKnowledge(token: string, body: DocumentInput, file: File) {
  const form = new FormData()
  form.append('file', file)
  form.append('title', body.title)
  form.append('product_id', String(body.product_id))
  form.append('module_id', String(body.module_id))
  form.append('category_id', String(body.category_id))
  return request('/knowledge/upload', documentSchema, { token, method: 'POST', body: form })
}
export function changeKnowledge(token: string, id: number, action: 'publish' | 'disable') {
  return request(`/knowledge/${id}/${action}`, documentSchema, { token, method: 'POST' })
}
export function searchKnowledge(token: string, ticketId: number) {
  return request(`/tickets/${ticketId}/knowledge`, knowledgeResultSchema,
    { token, method: 'POST', timeoutMs: 60000 })
}
```

### api/models.ts

```typescript
import { z } from 'zod'

export const userSchema = z.object({
  id: z.number().int().positive(),
  username: z.string(),
  display_name: z.string(),
  role: z.enum(['admin', 'staff']),
  is_active: z.boolean(),
})
export type User = z.infer<typeof userSchema>

export const namedSchema = z.object({
  id: z.number().int().positive(),
  name: z.string(),
  is_active: z.boolean(),
})
export const moduleSchema = namedSchema.extend({ product_id: z.number().int().positive() })
export type NamedItem = z.infer<typeof namedSchema>
export type ProductModule = z.infer<typeof moduleSchema>

export const prioritySchema = z.enum(['P1', 'P2', 'P3', 'P4'])
export const statusSchema = z.enum(['pending', 'processing', 'resolved', 'closed'])
export const statusLabels: Record<z.infer<typeof statusSchema>, string> = {
  pending: '待处理', processing: '处理中', resolved: '已解决', closed: '已关闭',
}

export const ticketSchema = z.object({
  id: z.number().int().positive(),
  number: z.string(),
  title: z.string(),
  description: z.string(),
  customer_name: z.string(),
  customer_email: z.string(),
  product_id: z.number().int().positive(),
  module_id: z.number().int().positive(),
  category_id: z.number().int().positive(),
  owner_id: z.number().int().positive(),
  priority: prioritySchema,
  status: statusSchema,
  solution: z.string(),
  created_at: z.string(),
  updated_at: z.string(),
})
export const ticketDetailSchema = ticketSchema.extend({
  records: z.array(z.object({
    id: z.number().int().positive(), author_id: z.number().int().positive(),
    kind: z.string(), content: z.string(), created_at: z.string(),
  })),
})
export const ticketPageSchema = z.object({
  items: z.array(ticketSchema), total: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  page: z.number().int().positive(), page_size: z.number().int().positive(),
})
export type Ticket = z.infer<typeof ticketSchema>
export type TicketDetail = z.infer<typeof ticketDetailSchema>
export type TicketInput = Pick<Ticket,
  'title' | 'description' | 'customer_name' | 'customer_email' |
  'product_id' | 'module_id' | 'category_id' | 'priority'
>
```

### api/tickets.ts

`frontend/src/api/tickets.ts`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

组件的导入名称及样式路径按 Element Plus 规定填写；页面标题和提示文案可改，接口字段和事件名称要与调用方一致。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/api/tickets.ts -->
```typescript
import { request } from './http'
import { ticketDetailSchema, ticketPageSchema } from './models'
import type { Ticket, TicketInput } from './models'

export interface TicketFilters {
  page: number
  keyword?: string
  status?: Ticket['status']
  priority?: Ticket['priority']
  product_id?: number
  module_id?: number
  category_id?: number
  owner_id?: number
}

export function listTickets(token: string, filters: TicketFilters) {
  const query = new URLSearchParams()
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(filters)) {
    if (value !== undefined && value !== '') query.set(key, String(value))
  }
  return request('/tickets?' + query.toString(), ticketPageSchema, { token })
}

export const getTicket = (token: string, id: number) =>
  request(`/tickets/${id}`, ticketDetailSchema, { token })

export const createTicket = (token: string, body: TicketInput) =>
  request('/tickets', ticketDetailSchema, { token, method: 'POST', body })

export const updateTicket = (token: string, id: number, body: TicketInput & { owner_id: number }) =>
  request(`/tickets/${id}`, ticketDetailSchema, { token, method: 'PATCH', body })

export const startTicket = (token: string, id: number) =>
  request(`/tickets/${id}/start`, ticketDetailSchema, { token, method: 'POST' })

export const addNote = (token: string, id: number, content: string) =>
  request(`/tickets/${id}/records`, ticketDetailSchema, { token, method: 'POST', body: { content } })

export const resolveTicket = (token: string, id: number, solution: string) =>
  request(`/tickets/${id}/resolve`, ticketDetailSchema, { token, method: 'POST', body: { solution } })

export const closeTicket = (token: string, id: number) =>
  request(`/tickets/${id}/close`, ticketDetailSchema, {
    token, method: 'POST', body: { customer_reply_confirmed: true },
  })
```

```typescript
import { z } from 'zod'
```

### components/AssistancePanel.vue

`frontend/src/components/AssistancePanel.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

组件的导入名称及样式路径按 Element Plus 规定填写；页面标题和提示文案可改，接口字段和事件名称要与调用方一致。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/components/AssistancePanel.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ElButton, ElCheckbox } from 'element-plus'
import 'element-plus/es/components/button/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/checkbox/style/css'
import { onMounted, onUnmounted, ref } from 'vue'
import { adoptAssistance, generateAssistance, listAssistance, type Assistance } from '../api/assistance'
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth'

const props = defineProps<{ ticketId: number; status: string; categories: Record<number, string> }>()
const emit = defineEmits<{ adopted: []; preview: [source: { kind: 'ticket' | 'knowledge'; id: number }] }>()
const auth = useAuthStore()
const current = ref<Assistance | null>(null)
const history = ref<Assistance[]>([])
const page = ref(1)
const solution = ref('')
const reply = ref('')
const confirmed = ref(false)
const busy = ref(false)
const generating = ref(false)
const elapsed = ref(0)
let timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | undefined
onUnmounted(() => clearInterval(timer))
const errorMessage = ref('')
function select(run: Assistance) {
  current.value = run
  solution.value = run.final_solution ?? run.draft.steps.map((step, i) => `${i + 1}. ${step.instruction} [${step.source_ids.join(', ')}]`).join('\n')
  reply.value = run.final_reply ?? `主题：${run.draft.reply_subject}\n\n${run.draft.reply_body}`
  confirmed.value = false
}
async function load() {
  history.value = await listAssistance(auth.token, props.ticketId, page.value)
}
onMounted(async () => {
  try {
    await load()
    if (history.value[0]) select(history.value[0])
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  }
})
async function perform(action: 'generate' | 'adopt' | 'history') {
  if (busy.value) return
  busy.value = true
  errorMessage.value = ''
  try {
    if (action === 'generate') {
      generating.value = true
      elapsed.value = 0
      const started = Date.now()
      timer = setInterval(() => { elapsed.value = Math.floor((Date.now() - started) / 1000) }, 1000)
      select(await generateAssistance(auth.token, props.ticketId))
      page.value = 1
      await load()
    } else if (action === 'adopt' && current.value) {
      select(await adoptAssistance(auth.token, props.ticketId, current.value.id, solution.value, reply.value))
      emit('adopted')
      await load()
    } else { await load() }
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  } finally { clearInterval(timer); generating.value = false; busy.value = false }
}
</script>

<template>
  <section class="panel assistance-panel">
    <div class="eyebrow">智能辅助 / 人工确认</div>
    <h2>AI 处理建议</h2>
    <p>基于真实检索依据生成，需人工核实；采用后保存处理记录和回复草稿。</p>
    <el-button type="primary" :disabled="busy || !['pending', 'processing'].includes(status)" @click="perform('generate')">生成处理建议</el-button>
    <p v-if="generating" role="status">正在生成，已等待 {{ elapsed }} 秒。{{ current ? '下方仍是上一次结果；完成后替换。' : '完成后显示新结果。' }}</p>
    <p v-else-if="busy" role="status">正在保存或读取记录……</p>
    <p v-if="errorMessage" role="alert" class="error">{{ errorMessage }}</p>
    <template v-if="current">
      <p class="muted">#{{ current.id }} · {{ current.model }} · {{ current.prompt_version }} · {{ current.duration_ms }} ms · {{ current.created_at }}</p>
      <h3>问题摘要与判断</h3>
      <p>{{ current.draft.summary }}</p><p>{{ current.draft.diagnosis }}</p>
      <p>建议分类：{{ categories[current.draft.category_id] }} · 建议优先级：{{ current.draft.priority }}</p>
      <p>{{ current.draft.reason }}</p>
      <ol><li v-for="(step, i) in current.draft.steps" :key="i">{{ step.instruction }} [{{ step.source_ids.join(', ') }}]</li></ol>
      <h3>风险</h3><p v-if="!current.draft.risks.length">模型未列出风险，请自行核实。</p>
      <ul><li v-for="item in current.draft.risks" :key="item">{{ item }}</li></ul>
      <h3>需要确认</h3><ul><li v-for="item in current.draft.confirmations" :key="item">{{ item }}</li></ul>
      <h3>参考来源快照</h3>
      <article v-for="source in current.sources" :key="source.source_id" class="assistance-source">
        <button class="text-button" type="button" @click="emit('preview', { kind: source.kind, id: source.entity_id })">[{{ source.source_id }}] {{ source.title }}</button>
        <p class="multiline">{{ source.content }}</p>
      </article>
      <h3>人工编辑并确认</h3>
      <p>以下编辑保存为采用记录；分类、优先级和已解决状态通过工单原有按钮确认。</p>
      <label class="field">处理方案<textarea v-model="solution" rows="6" maxlength="10000" style="width:100%" :disabled="busy || !!current.adopted_at" /></label>
      <label class="field">回复草稿<textarea v-model="reply" rows="6" maxlength="6000" style="width:100%" :disabled="busy || !!current.adopted_at" /></label>
      <p v-if="current.adopted_at">已于 {{ current.adopted_at }} 人工确认采用；邮件仍需在系统外发送。</p>
      <template v-else>
        <p><el-checkbox v-model="confirmed" :disabled="busy">我已核对工单、引用依据和待确认信息，确认保存采用记录</el-checkbox></p>
        <el-button type="primary" :disabled="busy || !confirmed || !solution.trim() || !reply.trim() || !['pending', 'processing'].includes(status)" @click="perform('adopt')">确认采用并保存记录</el-button>
      </template>
    </template>
    <h3>生成历史（每页10条）</h3>
    <p v-if="!history.length && !busy" class="muted">还没有生成记录。</p>
    <ul><li v-for="run in history" :key="run.id"><button class="text-button" type="button" :disabled="busy" @click="select(run)">#{{ run.id }} · {{ run.created_at }} · {{ run.adopted_at ? '已采用' : '未采用' }}</button></li></ul>
    <el-button :disabled="busy || page === 1" @click="page--; perform('history')">上一页</el-button>
    <el-button :disabled="busy || history.length < 10" @click="page++; perform('history')">下一页</el-button>
  </section>
</template>
```

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ElDrawer, ElSkeleton } from 'element-plus'
```

### stores/auth.ts

`frontend/src/stores/auth.ts`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

组件的导入名称及样式路径按 Element Plus 规定填写；页面标题和提示文案可改，接口字段和事件名称要与调用方一致。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/stores/auth.ts -->
```typescript
import { defineStore } from 'pinia'
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { getCurrentUser, login } from '../api/users'
import { ApiError } from '../api/http'
import type { User } from '../api/models'

const SESSION_TOKEN_KEY = 'oshino.access_token'

export const useAuthStore = defineStore('auth', () => {
  // 当前标签页刷新后保留令牌；用户身份和角色仍由后端确认。
  const token = ref(sessionStorage.getItem(SESSION_TOKEN_KEY) ?? '')
  const user = ref<User | null>(null)

  async function signIn(username: string, password: string) {
    const result = await login(username, password)
    sessionStorage.setItem(SESSION_TOKEN_KEY, result.access_token)
    token.value = result.access_token
    user.value = result.user
  }

  async function restoreSession() {
    if (!token.value) return
    try {
      user.value = await getCurrentUser(token.value)
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 401) {
        signOut()
        return
      }
      throw error
    }
  }

  function signOut() {
    token.value = ''
    user.value = null
    sessionStorage.removeItem(SESSION_TOKEN_KEY)
  }

  return { token, user, signIn, signOut, restoreSession }
})
```

### views/AdminView.vue

`frontend/src/views/AdminView.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）
