<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/views/AdminView.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ElButton, ElCheckbox, ElInput, ElOption, ElRadioButton, ElRadioGroup, ElSelect, ElTable, ElTableColumn } from 'element-plus'
import 'element-plus/es/components/button/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/checkbox/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/input/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/option/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/radio-button/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/radio-group/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/select/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/table/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/table-column/style/css'
import { computed, onMounted, reactive, ref } from 'vue'
import { createConfig, getCatalog, updateConfig } from '../api/config'
import type { ConfigKind } from '../api/config'
import { createStaff, listUsers, setStaffActive } from '../api/users'
import type { NamedItem, User } from '../api/models'
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth'

const auth = useAuthStore()
const users = ref<User[]>([])
const catalog = ref<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getCatalog>>>({ products: [], modules: [], categories: [] })
const staffForm = reactive({ username: '', display_name: '', password: '' })
const configKind = ref<ConfigKind>('products')
const configName = ref('')
const configActive = ref(true)
const productId = ref<number>()
const editingId = ref<number | null>(null)
const rows = computed(() => catalog.value[configKind.value])
const productNames = computed(() => Object.fromEntries(catalog.value.products.map(item => [item.id, item.name])))
const busy = ref(false)
const errorMessage = ref('')

async function load() {
  const [members, config] = await Promise.all([listUsers(auth.token), getCatalog(auth.token)])
  users.value = members
  catalog.value = config
}

onMounted(async () => {
  busy.value = true
  try {
    await load()
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  } finally {
    busy.value = false
  }
})

function resetConfigForm() {
  editingId.value = null
  configName.value = ''
  configActive.value = true
  productId.value = undefined
}

function editConfig(item: NamedItem) {
  editingId.value = item.id
  configName.value = item.name
  configActive.value = item.is_active
}

async function saveConfig() {
  errorMessage.value = ''
  if (configKind.value === 'modules' && editingId.value === null && productId.value === undefined) {
    errorMessage.value = '请先选择模块所属产品'
    return
  }
  busy.value = true
  try {
    if (editingId.value === null) {
      await createConfig(auth.token, configKind.value, configName.value, productId.value)
    } else {
      await updateConfig(auth.token, configKind.value, editingId.value, configName.value, configActive.value)
    }
    resetConfigForm()
    await load()
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  } finally {
    busy.value = false
  }
}

async function saveStaff() {
  busy.value = true
  errorMessage.value = ''
  try {
    await createStaff(auth.token, staffForm)
    staffForm.username = ''
    staffForm.display_name = ''
    staffForm.password = ''
    await load()
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  } finally {
    busy.value = false
  }
}

async function toggleStaff(id: number, isActive: boolean) {
  busy.value = true
  errorMessage.value = ''
  try {
    await setStaffActive(auth.token, id, !isActive)
    await load()
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  } finally {
    busy.value = false
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <h1>基础管理</h1>
  <p v-if="errorMessage" role="alert" class="error">{{ errorMessage }}</p>
  <section class="panel" v-loading="busy">
    <h2>产品、模块与问题分类</h2>
    <el-radio-group v-model="configKind" @change="resetConfigForm">
      <el-radio-button value="products">产品</el-radio-button>
      <el-radio-button value="modules">模块</el-radio-button>
      <el-radio-button value="categories">问题分类</el-radio-button>
    </el-radio-group>
    <form class="filters" @submit.prevent="saveConfig">
      <el-input v-model="configName" placeholder="名称" required maxlength="100" />
      <el-select v-if="configKind === 'modules' && editingId === null" v-model="productId" placeholder="所属产品">
        <el-option v-for="item in catalog.products" :key="item.id" :label="item.name" :value="item.id" :disabled="!item.is_active" />
      </el-select>
      <el-checkbox v-if="editingId !== null" v-model="configActive">启用</el-checkbox>
      <el-button type="primary" native-type="submit" :disabled="busy">{{ editingId === null ? '新增' : '保存修改' }}</el-button>
      <el-button v-if="editingId !== null" @click="resetConfigForm">取消编辑</el-button>
    </form>
    <el-table :data="rows">
      <el-table-column prop="name" label="名称" />
      <el-table-column v-if="configKind === 'modules'" label="所属产品">
        <template #default="{ row }">{{ productNames[row.product_id] }}</template>
      </el-table-column>
      <el-table-column label="状态"><template #default="{ row }">{{ row.is_active ? '启用' : '停用' }}</template></el-table-column>
      <el-table-column label="操作"><template #default="{ row }"><el-button :disabled="busy" @click="editConfig({ id: row.id, name: row.name, is_active: row.is_active })">编辑</el-button></template></el-table-column>
    </el-table>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" v-loading="busy">
    <h2>客服账号</h2>
    <form class="filters" @submit.prevent="saveStaff">
      <el-input v-model="staffForm.username" placeholder="用户名：字母、数字、下划线" required maxlength="50" autocomplete="off" />
      <el-input v-model="staffForm.display_name" placeholder="显示姓名" required maxlength="100" />
      <el-input v-model="staffForm.password" type="password" placeholder="初始密码，至少 8 位" required minlength="8" maxlength="128" autocomplete="new-password" show-password />
      <el-button native-type="submit" type="primary" :disabled="busy">创建客服</el-button>
    </form>
    <el-table :data="users">
      <el-table-column prop="username" label="用户名" />
      <el-table-column prop="display_name" label="姓名" />
      <el-table-column label="角色"><template #default="{ row }">{{ row.role === 'admin' ? '管理员' : '客服' }}</template></el-table-column>
      <el-table-column label="状态"><template #default="{ row }">{{ row.is_active ? '启用' : '停用' }}</template></el-table-column>
      <el-table-column label="操作">
        <template #default="{ row }">
          <el-button v-if="row.role === 'staff'" :disabled="busy" @click="toggleStaff(row.id, row.is_active)">{{ row.is_active ? '停用' : '启用' }}</el-button>
        </template>
      </el-table-column>
    </el-table>
  </section>
</template>
```

### views/ImportView.vue

`frontend/src/views/ImportView.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

组件的导入名称及样式路径按 Element Plus 规定填写；页面标题和提示文案可改，接口字段和事件名称要与调用方一致。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/views/ImportView.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ElButton } from 'element-plus'
import 'element-plus/es/components/button/style/css'
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { importTickets } from '../api/imports'
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth'

const auth = useAuthStore()
const fileInput = ref<HTMLInputElement | null>(null)
const busy = ref(false)
const result = ref<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof importTickets>> | null>(null)
const errorMessage = ref('')

async function submit() {
  result.value = null
  errorMessage.value = ''
  const file = fileInput.value?.files?.[0]
  if (!file) {
    errorMessage.value = '请先选择 CSV 文件'
    return
  }
  if (file.size > 4 * 1024 * 1024) {
    errorMessage.value = 'CSV 不能超过 4 MiB'
    return
  }
  busy.value = true
  try {
    result.value = await importTickets(auth.token, file)
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  } finally {
    busy.value = false
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <section class="panel">
    <h1>导入历史工单</h1>
    <p>仅管理员可导入。UTF-8 CSV，最多 4 MiB、5000 条；任何记录有误时整批不写入。</p>
    <p>产品、模块、分类需先在基础管理中建立。import_key 是来源唯一编号，同一编号不能重复导入。</p>
    <p>pending 表示待处理，解决方案留空；resolved 表示已解决，必须有方案。负责人为导入管理员，时间为导入时间。</p>
    <a href="/import-template.csv" download>下载空白 CSV 模板</a>
    <form @submit.prevent="submit">
      <p><input ref="fileInput" type="file" accept=".csv,text/csv" aria-label="选择 CSV 文件" :disabled="busy" /></p>
      <el-button type="primary" native-type="submit" :loading="busy" :disabled="busy">导入工单</el-button>
    </form>
    <p v-if="errorMessage" class="error" role="alert">{{ errorMessage }}</p>
    <div v-if="result" role="status">
      <p>成功导入 {{ result.imported }} 条工单。</p>
      <RouterLink to="/tickets">到工单列表查看</RouterLink>
    </div>
    <p class="muted">请求超时时先查询列表确认结果，不要马上修改来源编号重新上传。</p>
  </section>
</template>
```

### views/KnowledgeEditor.vue

`frontend/src/views/KnowledgeEditor.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

组件的导入名称及样式路径按 Element Plus 规定填写；页面标题和提示文案可改，接口字段和事件名称要与调用方一致。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/views/KnowledgeEditor.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ElButton } from 'element-plus'
import 'element-plus/es/components/button/style/css'
import { computed, onMounted, reactive, ref } from 'vue'
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { getCatalog } from '../api/config'
import { changeKnowledge, getKnowledge, saveKnowledge, uploadKnowledge, type KnowledgeDocument } from '../api/knowledge'
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth'

const auth = useAuthStore()
const route = useRoute()
const router = useRouter()
const document = ref<KnowledgeDocument | null>(null)
const catalog = ref<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getCatalog>> | null>(null)
const form = reactive({ title: '', content: '', product_id: 0, module_id: 0, category_id: 0 })
const modules = computed(() => catalog.value?.modules.filter(item => item.product_id === form.product_id && item.is_active))
const file = ref<File | null>(null)
const ready = ref(false)
const busy = ref(false)
const errorMessage = ref('')
const notice = ref('')
onMounted(async () => {
  try {
    if (route.params.id) {
      document.value = await getKnowledge(auth.token, Number(route.params.id))
      Object.assign(form, document.value)
    }
    if (auth.user?.role === 'admin') catalog.value = await getCatalog(auth.token)
    ready.value = true
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  }
})
function selectFile(event: Event) {
  file.value = (event.target as HTMLInputElement).files?.[0] ?? null
}
async function save(action: 'save' | 'publish' | 'disable') {
  busy.value = true
  errorMessage.value = ''
  notice.value = ''
  try {
    if (action === 'save') {
      // 仅发送编辑字段，避免把读取响应的状态和编号一起提交。
      const body = { title: form.title, content: form.content, product_id: form.product_id,
        module_id: form.module_id, category_id: form.category_id }
      document.value = file.value && !document.value
        ? await uploadKnowledge(auth.token, body, file.value)
        : await saveKnowledge(auth.token, body, document.value?.id)
      Object.assign(form, document.value)
      file.value = null
      await router.replace('/knowledge/' + document.value.id)
      notice.value = '草稿已保存，请核对正文后发布。'
    } else if (document.value) {
      document.value = await changeKnowledge(auth.token, document.value.id, action)
      notice.value = action === 'publish' ? '已发布并生成分块。请停后端后运行知识索引重建命令。' : '已停用，后续检索会排除此文档。'
    }
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  } finally { busy.value = false }
}
</script>

<template>
  <section class="panel">
    <h1>{{ document?.title ?? '新增知识文档' }}</h1>
    <RouterLink to="/knowledge">返回知识列表</RouterLink>
    <p v-if="errorMessage" role="alert" class="error">{{ errorMessage }}</p>
    <p v-if="notice" role="status">{{ notice }}</p>
    <template v-if="ready">
      <p v-if="document">状态：{{ document.status }} · 第 {{ document.revision }} 版 · {{ document.chunk_count }} 块 · 来源：{{ document.source_name }}</p>
      <form v-if="auth.user?.role === 'admin' && catalog" @submit.prevent="save('save')">
        <p><label>标题 <input v-model="form.title" required maxlength="200" /></label></p>
        <p><label>产品 <select v-model.number="form.product_id" required @change="form.module_id = 0">
          <option disabled :value="0">请选择</option>
          <option v-for="item in catalog.products.filter(x => x.is_active)" :key="item.id" :value="item.id">{{ item.name }}</option>
        </select></label></p>
        <p><label>模块 <select v-model.number="form.module_id" required>
          <option disabled :value="0">请选择</option>
          <option v-for="item in modules" :key="item.id" :value="item.id">{{ item.name }}</option>
        </select></label></p>
        <p><label>分类 <select v-model.number="form.category_id" required>
          <option disabled :value="0">请选择</option>
          <option v-for="item in catalog.categories.filter(x => x.is_active)" :key="item.id" :value="item.id">{{ item.name }}</option>
        </select></label></p>
        <p v-if="!document"><label>可选：上传 Markdown / TXT <input type="file" accept=".md,.txt" @change="selectFile" /></label></p>
        <p>上传文件会作为草稿正文；已建文档请在下面编辑正文。Markdown 以纯文本显示。</p>
        <textarea v-model="form.content" :required="!file" rows="18" maxlength="100000" style="width:100%" aria-label="知识正文" />
        <p><el-button native-type="submit" :disabled="busy">保存草稿</el-button></p>
      </form>
      <pre v-else class="multiline">{{ document?.content }}</pre>
      <template v-if="auth.user?.role === 'admin' && document">
        <p>发布的是已保存的正文；修改后先保存，再发布。</p>
        <el-button :disabled="busy || document.status === 'published'" @click="save('publish')">发布已保存正文</el-button>
        <el-button :disabled="busy || document.status === 'disabled'" @click="save('disable')">停用</el-button>
      </template>
    </template>
  </section>
</template>
```

### views/KnowledgeView.vue

`frontend/src/views/KnowledgeView.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

组件的导入名称及样式路径按 Element Plus 规定填写；页面标题和提示文案可改，接口字段和事件名称要与调用方一致。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/views/KnowledgeView.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ElButton } from 'element-plus'
import 'element-plus/es/components/button/style/css'
import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue'
import { listKnowledge, type KnowledgeDocument } from '../api/knowledge'
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth'

const auth = useAuthStore()
const documents = ref<KnowledgeDocument[]>([])
const page = ref(1)
const total = ref(0)
const busy = ref(false)
const errorMessage = ref('')
async function load() {
  busy.value = true
  errorMessage.value = ''
  try {
    const result = await listKnowledge(auth.token, page.value)
    documents.value = result.items
    total.value = result.total
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  } finally { busy.value = false }
}
onMounted(load)
</script>

<template>
  <section class="panel">
    <h1>知识库</h1>
    <RouterLink v-if="auth.user?.role === 'admin'" to="/knowledge/new">录入或上传知识文档</RouterLink>
    <p v-if="errorMessage" role="alert" class="error">{{ errorMessage }}</p>
    <p v-if="busy">正在读取……</p>
    <ul><li v-for="doc in documents" :key="doc.id">
      <RouterLink :to="'/knowledge/' + doc.id">{{ doc.title }}</RouterLink>
      · {{ doc.status }} · 第 {{ doc.revision }} 版 · {{ doc.chunk_count }} 块
    </li></ul>
    <p>共 {{ total }} 篇，第 {{ page }} 页</p>
    <el-button :disabled="busy || page === 1" @click="page--; load()">上一页</el-button>
    <el-button :disabled="busy || page * 20 >= total" @click="page++; load()">下一页</el-button>
  </section>
</template>
```

### views/TicketEditor.vue

`frontend/src/views/TicketEditor.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

组件的导入名称及样式路径按 Element Plus 规定填写；页面标题和提示文案可改，接口字段和事件名称要与调用方一致。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/views/TicketEditor.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ElButton, ElFormItem, ElInput, ElOption, ElSelect } from 'element-plus'
import 'element-plus/es/components/button/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/form-item/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/input/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/option/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/select/style/css'
import { computed, onMounted, reactive, ref } from 'vue'
import { useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { getCatalog } from '../api/config'
import { listUsers } from '../api/users'
import { createTicket, getTicket, updateTicket } from '../api/tickets'
import type { Ticket, TicketInput, User } from '../api/models'
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth'

const auth = useAuthStore()
const route = useRoute()
const router = useRouter()
const ticketId = route.params.id ? Number(route.params.id) : null
const catalog = ref<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getCatalog>>>({ products: [], modules: [], categories: [] })
const users = ref<User[]>([])
const form = reactive({
  title: '', description: '', customer_name: '', customer_email: '',
  product_id: undefined as number | undefined,
  module_id: undefined as number | undefined,
  category_id: undefined as number | undefined,
  owner_id: undefined as number | undefined,
  priority: undefined as Ticket['priority'] | undefined,
})
const availableModules = computed(() => catalog.value.modules.filter(item => item.product_id === form.product_id))
const loading = ref(true)
const loaded = ref(false)
const submitting = ref(false)
const errorMessage = ref('')
const isClosed = ref(false)

onMounted(async () => {
  try {
    const [config, members] = await Promise.all([getCatalog(auth.token), listUsers(auth.token)])
    catalog.value = config
    users.value = members
    if (ticketId !== null) {
      const ticket = await getTicket(auth.token, ticketId)
      form.title = ticket.title
      form.description = ticket.description
      form.customer_name = ticket.customer_name
      form.customer_email = ticket.customer_email
      form.product_id = ticket.product_id
      form.module_id = ticket.module_id
      form.category_id = ticket.category_id
      form.owner_id = ticket.owner_id
      form.priority = ticket.priority
      isClosed.value = ticket.status === 'closed'
    }
    loaded.value = true
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
})

async function submit() {
  errorMessage.value = ''
  if (form.product_id === undefined || form.module_id === undefined ||
      form.category_id === undefined || form.priority === undefined) {
    errorMessage.value = '请选择产品、模块、分类和优先级'
    return
  }
  const body: TicketInput = {
    title: form.title, description: form.description,
    customer_name: form.customer_name, customer_email: form.customer_email,
    product_id: form.product_id, module_id: form.module_id,
    category_id: form.category_id, priority: form.priority,
  }
  submitting.value = true
  try {
    let saved
    if (ticketId === null) {
      saved = await createTicket(auth.token, body)
    } else {
      if (form.owner_id === undefined) {
        errorMessage.value = '请选择负责人'
        return
      }
      saved = await updateTicket(auth.token, ticketId, { ...body, owner_id: form.owner_id })
    }
    await router.push('/tickets/' + saved.id)
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  } finally {
    submitting.value = false
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <section class="panel" v-loading="loading">
    <h1>{{ ticketId === null ? '创建工单' : '编辑工单' }}</h1>
    <p v-if="isClosed">已关闭工单只允许查看。</p>
    <form v-else-if="loaded" class="form-grid" @submit.prevent="submit">
      <el-form-item label="标题" class="full"><el-input v-model="form.title" required maxlength="200" /></el-form-item>
      <el-form-item label="客户姓名"><el-input v-model="form.customer_name" required maxlength="100" /></el-form-item>
      <el-form-item label="客户邮箱"><el-input v-model="form.customer_email" type="email" required maxlength="320" /></el-form-item>
      <el-form-item label="产品">
        <el-select v-model="form.product_id" placeholder="请选择产品" @change="form.module_id = undefined">
          <el-option v-for="item in catalog.products" :key="item.id" :label="item.name" :value="item.id" :disabled="!item.is_active" />
        </el-select>
      </el-form-item>
      <el-form-item label="模块">
        <el-select v-model="form.module_id" placeholder="请选择模块">
          <el-option v-for="item in availableModules" :key="item.id" :label="item.name" :value="item.id" :disabled="!item.is_active" />
        </el-select>
      </el-form-item>
      <el-form-item label="问题分类">
        <el-select v-model="form.category_id" placeholder="请选择分类">
          <el-option v-for="item in catalog.categories" :key="item.id" :label="item.name" :value="item.id" :disabled="!item.is_active" />
        </el-select>
      </el-form-item>
      <el-form-item label="优先级">
        <el-select v-model="form.priority" placeholder="请选择优先级">
          <el-option v-for="priority in ['P1', 'P2', 'P3', 'P4']" :key="priority" :label="priority" :value="priority" />
        </el-select>
      </el-form-item>
      <el-form-item v-if="ticketId !== null" label="负责人">
        <el-select v-model="form.owner_id">
          <el-option v-for="item in users" :key="item.id" :label="item.display_name" :value="item.id" :disabled="!item.is_active" />
        </el-select>
      </el-form-item>
      <el-form-item label="问题描述" class="full">
        <el-input v-model="form.description" type="textarea" :rows="6" required maxlength="10000" />
      </el-form-item>
      <div class="actions full">
        <el-button type="primary" native-type="submit" :loading="submitting" :disabled="loading">保存工单</el-button>
        <RouterLink to="/tickets">返回列表</RouterLink>
      </div>
    </form>
    <p v-if="errorMessage" role="alert" class="error">{{ errorMessage }}</p>
  </section>
</template>
```

### views/TicketsView.vue

`frontend/src/views/TicketsView.vue`（源码见本教程对应代码块）

组件的导入名称及样式路径按 Element Plus 规定填写；页面标题和提示文案可改，接口字段和事件名称要与调用方一致。

<!-- source: 08-evaluation/frontend/src/views/TicketsView.vue -->
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ElButton, ElInput, ElOption, ElPagination, ElSelect, ElTable, ElTableColumn } from 'element-plus'
import 'element-plus/es/components/button/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/input/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/option/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/pagination/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/select/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/table/style/css'
import 'element-plus/es/components/table-column/style/css'
import { computed, nextTick, onActivated, reactive, ref } from 'vue'
import { onBeforeRouteLeave } from 'vue-router'
import { getCatalog } from '../api/config'
import { listUsers } from '../api/users'
import { listTickets } from '../api/tickets'
import type { TicketFilters } from '../api/tickets'
import type { Ticket, User } from '../api/models'
import { statusLabels } from '../api/models'
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth'

const auth = useAuthStore()
const listElement = ref<HTMLElement | null>(null)
let scrollTop = 0
onBeforeRouteLeave(() => {
  scrollTop = listElement.value?.closest('.page')?.scrollTop ?? 0
})
const filters = reactive<TicketFilters>({ page: 1 })
const catalog = ref<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getCatalog>>>({ products: [], modules: [], categories: [] })
const users = ref<User[]>([])
const tickets = ref<Ticket[]>([])
const total = ref(0)
const loading = ref(false)
const errorMessage = ref('')
const availableModules = computed(() => catalog.value.modules.filter(
  item => !filters.product_id || item.product_id === filters.product_id,
))
const productNames = computed(() => Object.fromEntries(catalog.value.products.map(item => [item.id, item.name])))
const ownerNames = computed(() => Object.fromEntries(users.value.map(item => [item.id, item.display_name])))

async function loadTickets() {
  loading.value = true
  errorMessage.value = ''
  try {
    const result = await listTickets(auth.token, filters)
    tickets.value = result.items
    total.value = result.total
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

async function search() {
  filters.page = 1
  await loadTickets()
}

onActivated(async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const [config, members] = await Promise.all([getCatalog(auth.token), listUsers(auth.token)])
    catalog.value = config
    users.value = members
    await loadTickets()
    await nextTick()
    listElement.value?.closest('.page')?.scrollTo({ top: scrollTop })
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error)) throw error
    errorMessage.value = error.message
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
})
</script>

<template>
  <section ref="listElement" class="panel">
    <div class="actions">
      <h1>工单列表</h1>
      <RouterLink to="/tickets/new"><el-button type="primary">创建工单</el-button></RouterLink>
    </div>
    <div class="filters">
      <el-input v-model="filters.keyword" placeholder="搜索标题或描述" clearable />
      <el-select v-model="filters.status" placeholder="全部状态" clearable>
        <el-option v-for="(label, value) in statusLabels" :key="value" :label="label" :value="value" />
      </el-select>
      <el-select v-model="filters.priority" placeholder="全部优先级" clearable>
        <el-option v-for="priority in ['P1', 'P2', 'P3', 'P4']" :key="priority" :label="priority" :value="priority" />
      </el-select>
      <el-select v-model="filters.product_id" placeholder="全部产品" clearable @change="filters.module_id = undefined">
        <el-option v-for="item in catalog.products" :key="item.id" :label="item.name" :value="item.id" />
      </el-select>
      <el-select v-model="filters.module_id" placeholder="全部模块" clearable>
        <el-option v-for="item in availableModules" :key="item.id" :label="item.name" :value="item.id" />
      </el-select>
      <el-select v-model="filters.category_id" placeholder="全部分类" clearable>
        <el-option v-for="item in catalog.categories" :key="item.id" :label="item.name" :value="item.id" />
      </el-select>
      <el-select v-model="filters.owner_id" placeholder="全部负责人" clearable>
        <el-option v-for="item in users" :key="item.id" :label="item.display_name" :value="item.id" />
      </el-select>
      <el-button :disabled="loading" @click="search">查询</el-button>
    </div>
    <p v-if="errorMessage" role="alert" class="error">{{ errorMessage }}</p>
    <el-table v-loading="loading" :data="tickets" empty-text="暂无工单">
      <el-table-column prop="number" label="编号" width="110" />
      <el-table-column prop="title" label="标题" min-width="200" />
      <el-table-column label="产品"><template #default="{ row }">{{ productNames[row.product_id] }}</template></el-table-column>
      <el-table-column prop="priority" label="优先级" width="90" />
      <el-table-column label="状态"><template #default="{ row }">{{ statusLabels[row.status as Ticket['status']] }}</template></el-table-column>
      <el-table-column label="负责人"><template #default="{ row }">{{ ownerNames[row.owner_id] }}</template></el-table-column>
      <el-table-column label="操作" width="90">
        <template #default="{ row }"><RouterLink :to="'/tickets/' + row.id">查看</RouterLink></template>
      </el-table-column>
    </el-table>
    <el-pagination v-model:current-page="filters.page" :total="total" :page-size="20"
      :disabled="loading" layout="prev, pager, next, total" @current-change="loadTickets" />
  </section>
</template>
```
